#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
repository_root="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$repository_root"
if [[ -z "${JAVA_HOME:-}" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' 'JAVA_HOME must point to JDK 25.' >&2
    exit 1
fi
"$JAVA_HOME/bin/java" -version
mvn -f JavaReports/pom.xml javafx:run


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\config\AppConfig.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.config;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public record AppConfig(String host, int port, String database, String appUser, String appPassword, String demoDirectorPassword, Path repositoryRoot) {
    public static AppConfig load() throws IOException {
        var root = findRepositoryRoot();
        var values = readEnvironment(root.resolve(".env"));
        var host = required(values, "PGHOST");
        var portValue = required(values, "PGPORT");
        int port;
        try {
            port = Integer.parseInt(portValue);
        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("PGPORT must be a valid TCP port.", exception);
        }
        if (port < 1 || port > 65535) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("PGPORT must be a valid TCP port.");
        }
        return new AppConfig(
            host,
            port,
            required(values, "PGDATABASE"),
            required(values, "APP_DB_USER"),
            required(values, "APP_DB_PASSWORD"),
            values.getOrDefault("DEMO_DIRECTOR_PASSWORD", ""),
            root
        );
    }

    private static Path findRepositoryRoot() {
        var current = Path.of(System.getProperty("user.dir")).toAbsolutePath();
        while (current != null) {
            if (Files.isRegularFile(current.resolve(".env")) && Files.isDirectory(current.resolve("commands"))) {
                return current;
            }
            current = current.getParent();
        }
        throw new IllegalStateException("The shared .env file was not found at the repository root.");
    }

    private static Map<String, String> readEnvironment(Path file) throws IOException {
        if (!Files.isRegularFile(file)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Create .env in the repository root from .env.example.");
        }
        var values = new HashMap<String, String>();
        for (var line : Files.readAllLines(file)) {
            var entry = line.trim();
            if (entry.isEmpty() || entry.startsWith("#")) {
                continue;
            }
            var separator = entry.indexOf('=');
            if (separator < 1) {
                continue;
            }
            var key = entry.substring(0, separator).trim();
            var value = entry.substring(separator + 1).trim();
            if (value.length() >= 2 && ((value.startsWith("\"") && value.endsWith("\"")) || (value.startsWith("'") && value.endsWith("'")))) {
                value = value.substring(1, value.length() - 1);
            }
            values.put(key, System.getenv().getOrDefault(key, value));
        }
        return values;
    }

    private static String required(Map<String, String> values, String key) {
        var value = values.get(key);
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Set " + key + " in the repository .env file.");
        }
        return value;
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\data\DirectorRepository.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.data;

import ua.zunu.reports.domain.Director;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Optional;

public interface DirectorRepository {
    Optional<Director> findByLogin(String login) throws SQLException;
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\data\JdbcDirectorRepository.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.data;

import ua.zunu.reports.domain.Director;
import ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure.DatabaseConnectionFactory;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Optional;

public final class JdbcDirectorRepository implements DirectorRepository {
    private final DatabaseConnectionFactory connections;

    public JdbcDirectorRepository(DatabaseConnectionFactory connections) {
        this.connections = connections;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<Director> findByLogin(String login) throws SQLException {
        var sql = "SELECT employee_id, first_name, last_name, login, role, password_hash FROM employee WHERE login = ?";
        try (var connection = connections.open(); var statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            statement.setString(1, login);
            try (var result = statement.executeQuery()) {
                if (!result.next()) {
                    return Optional.empty();
                }
                return Optional.of(new Director(
                    result.getInt("employee_id"),
                    result.getString("first_name"),
                    result.getString("last_name"),
                    result.getString("login"),
                    result.getString("role"),
                    result.getString("password_hash")
                ));
            }
        }
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\data\JdbcReportRepository.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.data;

import ua.zunu.reports.domain.DateRange;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportPoint;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportResult;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportType;
import ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure.DatabaseConnectionFactory;

import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class JdbcReportRepository implements ReportRepository {
    private final DatabaseConnectionFactory connections;

    public JdbcReportRepository(DatabaseConnectionFactory connections) {
        this.connections = connections;
    }

    @Override
    public ReportResult load(ReportType type, DateRange range) throws SQLException {
        var query = queryFor(type);
        var rows = new ArrayList<List<String>>();
        var points = new ArrayList<ReportPoint>();
        try (var connection = connections.open(); var statement = connection.prepareStatement(query.sql())) {
            statement.setObject(1, range.from());
            statement.setObject(2, range.to());
            try (var result = statement.executeQuery()) {
                while (result.next()) {
                    rows.add(readRow(result, query.columns().size()));
                    if (query.chartColumn() >= 0) {
                        var label = rows.getLast().get(query.labelColumn());
                        var value = rows.getLast().get(query.chartColumn());
                        points.add(new ReportPoint(label, parseNumber(value)));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return new ReportResult(type, range, query.columns(), rows, points, query.chartSeriesName());
    }

    private static List<String> readRow(ResultSet result, int columnCount) throws SQLException {
        var row = new ArrayList<String>(columnCount);
        for (var index = 1; index <= columnCount; index++) {
            var value = result.getString(index);
            row.add(value == null || value.isBlank() ? "—" : value);
        }
        return row;
    }

    private static double parseNumber(String value) {
        try {
            return Double.parseDouble(value.replace(',', '.').replaceAll("[^0-9.\\-]", ""));
        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    private static Query queryFor(ReportType type) {
        return switch (type) {
            case ORDERS -> new Query("""
                SELECT to_char(o.order_date, 'DD.MM.YYYY'),
                       '№ ' || c.car_id || ' · ' || c.model || ' · ' || c.license_plate,
                       concat_ws(' ', cu.last_name, cu.first_name),
                       concat_ws(' ', e.last_name, e.first_name),
                       CASE o.status WHEN 'open' THEN 'Нове' WHEN 'in_progress' THEN 'У роботі' WHEN 'closed' THEN 'Закрито' ELSE o.status END,
                       COALESCE(to_char(o.deadline, 'DD.MM.YYYY'), '—'),
                       o.mileage,
                       to_char(o.total_cost, 'FM999G999G990D00') || ' ₴'
                FROM orders o
                JOIN car c ON c.car_id = o.car_id
                JOIN customer cu ON cu.customer_id = c.customer_id
                JOIN employee e ON e.employee_id = o.employee_id
                WHERE o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                ORDER BY o.order_date DESC, o.order_id DESC
                """, List.of("Дата", "Автомобіль", "Клієнт", "Менеджер", "Статус", "Термін", "Пробіг, км", "Сума"), -1, -1, "");
            case EMPLOYEE_CARS -> new Query("""
                SELECT concat_ws(' ', e.last_name, e.first_name),
                       COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT '№ ' || c.car_id || ' · ' || c.model || ' · ' || c.license_plate, ', ' ORDER BY '№ ' || c.car_id || ' · ' || c.model || ' · ' || c.license_plate), '—'),
                       count(DISTINCT o.order_id),
                       COALESCE(sum(os.quantity) FILTER (WHERE o.order_id IS NOT NULL), 0)
                FROM employee e
                LEFT JOIN order_service os ON os.employee_id = e.employee_id
                LEFT JOIN orders o ON o.order_id = os.order_id AND o.status = 'closed' AND o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                LEFT JOIN car c ON c.car_id = o.car_id
                GROUP BY e.employee_id, e.last_name, e.first_name
                ORDER BY e.last_name, e.first_name
                """, List.of("Працівник", "Автомобілі", "Закриті замовлення", "Роботи виконано"), -1, -1, "");
            case SERVICE_COUNTS -> new Query("""
                SELECT s.name, COALESCE(sum(os.quantity), 0)::bigint
                FROM service s
                LEFT JOIN order_service os ON os.service_id = s.service_id
                LEFT JOIN orders o ON o.order_id = os.order_id AND o.status = 'closed' AND o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                GROUP BY s.service_id, s.name
                ORDER BY COALESCE(sum(os.quantity) FILTER (WHERE o.order_id IS NOT NULL), 0) DESC, s.name
                """, List.of("Назва послуги", "Кількість виконань"), 1, 0, "Виконання");
            case MONTHLY_REVENUE -> new Query("""
                SELECT to_char(date_trunc('month', o.order_date), 'YYYY-MM'),
                       to_char(sum(o.total_cost), 'FM999G999G990D00'),
                       count(*)
                FROM orders o
                WHERE o.status = 'closed' AND o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                GROUP BY date_trunc('month', o.order_date)
                ORDER BY date_trunc('month', o.order_date)
                """, List.of("Місяць", "Дохід, грн", "Замовлень закрито"), 1, 0, "Дохід, грн");
            case ORDER_STATUSES -> new Query("""
                SELECT CASE status WHEN 'open' THEN 'Нове' WHEN 'in_progress' THEN 'У роботі' WHEN 'closed' THEN 'Закрито' ELSE status END,
                       count(*),
                       to_char(sum(total_cost), 'FM999G999G990D00')
                FROM orders
                WHERE order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                GROUP BY status
                ORDER BY count(*) DESC
                """, List.of("Статус", "Замовлень", "Сума, грн"), 1, 0, "Замовлення");
            case TOP_SERVICES -> new Query("""
                SELECT s.name, sum(os.quantity), count(DISTINCT o.order_id), to_char(sum(os.quantity * os.price), 'FM999G999G990D00')
                FROM service s
                JOIN order_service os ON os.service_id = s.service_id
                JOIN orders o ON o.order_id = os.order_id
                WHERE o.status = 'closed' AND o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                GROUP BY s.service_id, s.name
                ORDER BY sum(os.quantity) DESC, sum(os.quantity * os.price) DESC
                LIMIT 20
                """, List.of("Послуга", "Виконань", "Замовлень", "Виручка, грн"), 1, 0, "Виконання");
            case PART_USAGE -> new Query("""
                SELECT p.name, p.article, sum(op.quantity), count(DISTINCT o.order_id), to_char(sum(op.quantity * op.price), 'FM999G999G990D00')
                FROM part p
                JOIN order_part op ON op.part_id = p.part_id
                JOIN orders o ON o.order_id = op.order_id
                WHERE o.status = 'closed' AND o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                GROUP BY p.part_id, p.name, p.article
                ORDER BY sum(op.quantity) DESC, p.name
                """, List.of("Запчастина", "Артикул", "Кількість", "Замовлень", "Вартість, грн"), 2, 0, "Використано");
            case EMPLOYEE_PERFORMANCE -> new Query("""
                SELECT concat_ws(' ', e.last_name, e.first_name), count(DISTINCT o.order_id),
                       sum(os.quantity), to_char(sum(os.quantity * os.price), 'FM999G999G990D00')
                FROM employee e
                JOIN order_service os ON os.employee_id = e.employee_id
                JOIN orders o ON o.order_id = os.order_id
                WHERE e.role = 'manager' AND o.status = 'closed' AND o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                GROUP BY e.employee_id, e.last_name, e.first_name
                ORDER BY sum(os.quantity * os.price) DESC
                """, List.of("Працівник", "Замовлень", "Робіт", "Виручка, грн"), 2, 0, "Робіт виконано");
            case CUSTOMER_ACTIVITY -> new Query("""
                SELECT concat_ws(' ', cu.last_name, cu.first_name), count(DISTINCT o.order_id),
                       count(DISTINCT c.car_id), to_char(sum(o.total_cost), 'FM999G999G990D00'),
                       to_char(max(o.order_date), 'DD.MM.YYYY')
                FROM customer cu
                JOIN car c ON c.customer_id = cu.customer_id
                JOIN orders o ON o.car_id = c.car_id
                WHERE o.status = 'closed' AND o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                GROUP BY cu.customer_id, cu.last_name, cu.first_name
                ORDER BY sum(o.total_cost) DESC
                LIMIT 30
                """, List.of("Клієнт", "Візитів", "Автомобілів", "Витрати, грн", "Останній візит"), 1, 0, "Візитів");
            case OVERDUE_ORDERS -> new Query("""
                SELECT o.order_id, to_char(o.order_date, 'DD.MM.YYYY'),
                       '№ ' || c.car_id || ' · ' || c.model || ' · ' || c.license_plate,
                       concat_ws(' ', cu.last_name, cu.first_name),
                       to_char(o.deadline, 'DD.MM.YYYY'), CURRENT_DATE - o.deadline,
                       to_char(o.total_cost, 'FM999G999G990D00') || ' ₴'
                FROM orders o
                JOIN car c ON c.car_id = o.car_id
                JOIN customer cu ON cu.customer_id = c.customer_id
                WHERE o.status IN ('open', 'in_progress') AND o.deadline < CURRENT_DATE AND o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                ORDER BY o.deadline, o.order_id
                """, List.of("№", "Дата", "Автомобіль", "Клієнт", "Термін", "Днів прострочення", "Сума"), -1, -1, "");
            case MILEAGE_PROGRESS -> new Query("""
                WITH mileage AS (
                    SELECT o.car_id, o.mileage, o.order_date, c.model, c.license_plate,
                           row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY o.car_id ORDER BY o.order_date, o.order_id) AS first_order,
                           row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY o.car_id ORDER BY o.order_date DESC, o.order_id DESC) AS last_order,
                           count(*) OVER (PARTITION BY o.car_id) AS visits
                    FROM orders o JOIN car c ON c.car_id = o.car_id
                    WHERE o.order_date BETWEEN ? AND ?
                )
                SELECT '№ ' || car_id || ' · ' || model || ' · ' || license_plate,
                       max(mileage) FILTER (WHERE first_order = 1),
                       max(mileage) FILTER (WHERE last_order = 1),
                       max(mileage) FILTER (WHERE last_order = 1) - max(mileage) FILTER (WHERE first_order = 1),
                       max(visits)
                FROM mileage
                GROUP BY car_id, model, license_plate
                HAVING max(visits) > 1
                ORDER BY max(mileage) FILTER (WHERE last_order = 1) - max(mileage) FILTER (WHERE first_order = 1) DESC
                """, List.of("Автомобіль", "Перший пробіг", "Останній пробіг", "Зміна, км", "Візитів"), 3, 0, "Зміна пробігу, км");
        };
    }

    private record Query(String sql, List<String> columns, int chartColumn, int labelColumn, String chartSeriesName) {
    }
}

package ua.zunu.reports.domain;

import java.time.LocalDate;

public record DateRange(LocalDate from, LocalDate to) {
    public DateRange {
        if (from == null || to == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Вкажіть початкову та кінцеву дати.");
        }
        if (from.isAfter(to)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Початкова дата не може бути пізніше кінцевої.");
        }
    }
}

package ua.zunu.reports.domain;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

public record ReportResult(ReportType type, DateRange range, List<String> columns, List<List<String>> rows, List<ReportPoint> chartPoints, String chartSeriesName) {
    public ReportResult {
        columns = List.copyOf(columns);
        rows = rows.stream().map(List::copyOf).toList();
        chartPoints = List.copyOf(chartPoints);
        chartSeriesName = chartSeriesName == null ? "" : chartSeriesName;
    }

    public String periodLabel() {
        return range.from() + " — " + range.to();
    }
}

package ua.zunu.reports.domain;

public enum ReportType {
    ORDERS("Журнал замовлень", "Замовлення за обраний період із клієнтом, авто, пробігом і сумою.", ChartType.NONE),
    EMPLOYEE_CARS("Працівники та автомобілі", "Автомобілі й кількість виконаних замовлень кожного працівника.", ChartType.NONE),
    SERVICE_COUNTS("Виконані послуги", "Кількість виконань кожної послуги за період.", ChartType.BAR),
    MONTHLY_REVENUE("Дохід за місяцями", "Чистий дохід та кількість закритих замовлень у динаміці.", ChartType.LINE),
    ORDER_STATUSES("Статуси замовлень", "Розподіл замовлень і їх вартості за статусами.", ChartType.BAR),
    TOP_SERVICES("Популярні послуги", "Найпопулярніші роботи за кількістю та сумою.", ChartType.BAR),
    PART_USAGE("Використання запчастин", "Кількість і вартість запчастин, використаних у закритих замовленнях.", ChartType.BAR),
    EMPLOYEE_PERFORMANCE("Результативність працівників", "Виконані роботи, замовлення та виручка за виконавцями.", ChartType.BAR),
    CUSTOMER_ACTIVITY("Активність клієнтів", "Кількість відвідувань, витрати та дата останнього візиту.", ChartType.BAR),
    OVERDUE_ORDERS("Прострочені замовлення", "Відкриті замовлення, термін яких уже минув.", ChartType.NONE),
    MILEAGE_PROGRESS("Пробіг автомобілів", "Зміна зафіксованого пробігу між першим та останнім замовленням.", ChartType.BAR);

    private final String title;
    private final String description;
    private final ChartType chartType;

    ReportType(String title, String description, ChartType chartType) {
        this.title = title;
        this.description = description;
        this.chartType = chartType;
    }

    public String title() {
        return title;
    }

    public String description() {
        return description;
    }

    public ChartType chartType() {
        return chartType;
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\export\ReportChartPainter.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.export;

import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ChartType;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportPoint;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportResult;

import java.awt.BasicStroke;
import java.awt.Color;
import java.awt.Font;
import java.awt.RenderingHints;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.util.List;

public final class ReportChartPainter {
    private static final Color INK = new Color(24, 34, 56);
    private static final Color BLUE = new Color(59, 112, 232);
    private static final Color MUTED = new Color(123, 137, 155);
    private static final Color GRID = new Color(229, 234, 241);

    public BufferedImage render(ReportResult result, int width, int height) {
        var image = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB);
        var graphics = image.createGraphics();
        graphics.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_ANTIALIASING, RenderingHints.VALUE_ANTIALIAS_ON);
        graphics.setColor(Color.WHITE);
        graphics.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
        var points = result.chartPoints().stream().limit(14).toList();
        if (points.isEmpty()) {
            graphics.dispose();
            return image;
        }
        var left = 54;
        var right = width - 24;
        var top = 24;
        var bottom = height - 52;
        graphics.setFont(new Font("SansSerif", Font.PLAIN, 11));
        graphics.setColor(MUTED);
        var max = Math.max(1, points.stream().mapToDouble(ReportPoint::value).max().orElse(1));
        for (var line = 0; line <= 4; line++) {
            var y = top + (bottom - top) * line / 4;
            graphics.setColor(GRID);
            graphics.drawLine(left, y, right, y);
            graphics.setColor(MUTED);
            graphics.drawString(shortValue(max * (4 - line) / 4), 8, y + 4);
        }
        if (result.type().chartType() == ChartType.LINE) {
            drawLine(points, graphics, left, right, top, bottom, max);
        } else {
            drawBars(points, graphics, left, right, top, bottom, max);
        }
        graphics.dispose();
        return image;
    }

    private static void drawBars(List<ReportPoint> points, java.awt.Graphics2D graphics, int left, int right, int top, int bottom, double max) {
        var slot = (right - left) / (double) points.size();
        for (var index = 0; index < points.size(); index++) {
            var point = points.get(index);
            var barHeight = (bottom - top) * Math.max(0, point.value()) / max;
            var x = (int) (left + slot * index + slot * .18);
            var width = Math.max(8, (int) (slot * .64));
            graphics.setColor(BLUE);
            graphics.fillRoundRect(x, (int) (bottom - barHeight), width, (int) barHeight, 8, 8);
            drawLabel(point.label(), graphics, x + width / 2, bottom + 18, slot);
        }
    }

    private static void drawLine(List<ReportPoint> points, java.awt.Graphics2D graphics, int left, int right, int top, int bottom, double max) {
        var slot = (right - left) / (double) Math.max(1, points.size() - 1);
        graphics.setColor(BLUE);
        graphics.setStroke(new BasicStroke(3, BasicStroke.CAP_ROUND, BasicStroke.JOIN_ROUND));
        var previousX = 0;
        var previousY = 0;
        for (var index = 0; index < points.size(); index++) {
            var x = (int) (left + slot * index);
            var y = (int) (bottom - (bottom - top) * Math.max(0, points.get(index).value()) / max);
            if (index > 0) {
                graphics.drawLine(previousX, previousY, x, y);
            }
            graphics.fillOval(x - 4, y - 4, 8, 8);
            drawLabel(points.get(index).label(), graphics, x, bottom + 18, slot);
            previousX = x;
            previousY = y;
        }
    }

    private static void drawLabel(String label, java.awt.Graphics2D graphics, int center, int y, double slot) {
        var clipped = label.length() > 14 ? label.substring(0, 13) + "…" : label;
        var textWidth = graphics.getFontMetrics().stringWidth(clipped);
        graphics.setColor(MUTED);
        graphics.drawString(clipped, center - Math.min(textWidth, (int) slot) / 2, y);
    }

    private static String shortValue(double value) {
        if (value >= 1_000_000) {
            return String.format("%.1fM", value / 1_000_000);
        }
        if (value >= 1_000) {
            return String.format("%.1fK", value / 1_000);
        }
        return String.format("%.0f", value);
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\export\ReportPdfExporter.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.export;

import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDPage;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDPageContentStream;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.common.PDRectangle;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.graphics.image.LosslessFactory;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.font.PDType0Font;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportResult;

import java.awt.Color;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class ReportPdfExporter {
    private static final float MARGIN = 38;
    private static final float FONT_SIZE = 8.5f;
    private static final float LINE_HEIGHT = 11f;
    private final ReportChartPainter charts = new ReportChartPainter();

    public void export(ReportResult report, Path file) throws IOException {
        var parent = file.toAbsolutePath().getParent();
        if (parent != null) {
            Files.createDirectories(parent);
        }
        try (var document = new PDDocument()) {
            var font = PDType0Font.load(document, findFont().toFile());
            var pageSize = new PDRectangle(PDRectangle.A4.getHeight(), PDRectangle.A4.getWidth());
            var widths = columnWidths(report, font, pageSize.getWidth() - 2 * MARGIN);
            var pageNumber = 1;
            var page = new PDPage(pageSize);
            document.addPage(page);
            var stream = new PDPageContentStream(document, page);
            var y = drawPageHeading(stream, document, pageSize, font, report, pageNumber, true);
            y = drawTableHeader(stream, font, report.columns(), widths, y);
            for (var rowIndex = 0; rowIndex < report.rows().size(); rowIndex++) {
                var row = report.rows().get(rowIndex);
                var lines = wrapRow(row, widths, font);
                var rowHeight = Math.max(25, lines.stream().mapToInt(List::size).max().orElse(1) * LINE_HEIGHT + 12);
                if (y - rowHeight < MARGIN) {
                    stream.close();
                    pageNumber++;
                    page = new PDPage(pageSize);
                    document.addPage(page);
                    stream = new PDPageContentStream(document, page);
                    y = drawPageHeading(stream, document, pageSize, font, report, pageNumber, false);
                    y = drawTableHeader(stream, font, report.columns(), widths, y);
                }
                y = drawRow(stream, font, row, lines, widths, y, rowIndex % 2 == 0, rowHeight);
            }
            if (report.rows().isEmpty()) {
                write(stream, font, "За обраний період записів немає.", MARGIN + 8, y - 20, FONT_SIZE + 1, new Color(103, 116, 134));
            }
            stream.close();
            document.save(file.toFile());
        }
    }

    private float drawPageHeading(PDPageContentStream stream, PDDocument document, PDRectangle pageSize, PDType0Font font, ReportResult report, int pageNumber, boolean includeChart) throws IOException {
        var y = pageSize.getHeight() - MARGIN;
        write(stream, font, "GARAGEFLOW  /  ЗВІТИ СЕРВІСНОГО ЦЕНТРУ", MARGIN, y, 8, new Color(80, 96, 119));
        write(stream, font, "Сторінка " + pageNumber, pageSize.getWidth() - MARGIN - 60, y, 8, new Color(80, 96, 119));
        y -= 30;
        write(stream, font, report.type().title(), MARGIN, y, 18, new Color(24, 34, 56));
        y -= 19;
        write(stream, font, report.type().description(), MARGIN, y, 9, new Color(103, 116, 134));
        y -= 16;
        write(stream, font, "Період: " + report.periodLabel() + "     Сформовано: " + LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm")), MARGIN, y, 8, new Color(103, 116, 134));
        y -= 20;
        if (includeChart && !report.chartPoints().isEmpty()) {
            var chart = charts.render(report, 1050, 260);
            var chartImage = LosslessFactory.createFromImage(document, chart);
            var imageWidth = Math.min(390, pageSize.getWidth() - 2 * MARGIN);
            var imageHeight = imageWidth * chart.getHeight() / chart.getWidth();
            stream.drawImage(chartImage, MARGIN, y - imageHeight, imageWidth, imageHeight);
            y -= imageHeight + 15;
        }
        return y;
    }

    private static float drawTableHeader(PDPageContentStream stream, PDType0Font font, List<String> columns, float[] widths, float y) throws IOException {
        var height = 25f;
        var x = MARGIN;
        for (var index = 0; index < columns.size(); index++) {
            stream.setNonStrokingColor(new Color(24, 34, 56));
            stream.addRect(x, y - height, widths[index], height);
            stream.fill();
            write(stream, font, fit(columns.get(index), font, 8, widths[index] - 12), x + 6, y - 16, 8, Color.WHITE);
            x += widths[index];
        }
        return y - height;
    }

    private static float drawRow(PDPageContentStream stream, PDType0Font font, List<String> row, List<List<String>> lines, float[] widths, float y, boolean alternate, float height) throws IOException {
        var x = MARGIN;
        for (var index = 0; index < widths.length; index++) {
            stream.setNonStrokingColor(alternate ? new Color(248, 250, 253) : Color.WHITE);
            stream.addRect(x, y - height, widths[index], height);
            stream.fill();
            stream.setStrokingColor(new Color(226, 231, 239));
            stream.addRect(x, y - height, widths[index], height);
            stream.stroke();
            var lineY = y - 15;
            for (var text : lines.get(index)) {
                write(stream, font, text, x + 6, lineY, FONT_SIZE, new Color(37, 50, 72));
                lineY -= LINE_HEIGHT;
            }
            x += widths[index];
        }
        return y - height;
    }

    private static float[] columnWidths(ReportResult report, PDType0Font font, float totalWidth) throws IOException {
        var weights = new float[report.columns().size()];
        var total = 0f;
        for (var column = 0; column < weights.length; column++) {
            var max = font.getStringWidth(report.columns().get(column)) / 1000 * FONT_SIZE;
            for (var row = 0; row < Math.min(report.rows().size(), 25); row++) {
                max = Math.max(max, Math.min(220, font.getStringWidth(report.rows().get(row).get(column)) / 1000 * FONT_SIZE));
            }
            weights[column] = Math.max(40, Math.min(max + 18, 190));
            total += weights[column];
        }
        var widths = new float[weights.length];
        for (var index = 0; index < widths.length; index++) {
            widths[index] = totalWidth * weights[index] / total;
        }
        return widths;
    }

    private static List<List<String>> wrapRow(List<String> row, float[] widths, PDType0Font font) throws IOException {
        var result = new ArrayList<List<String>>();
        for (var index = 0; index < row.size(); index++) {
            result.add(wrap(row.get(index), font, widths[index] - 12, FONT_SIZE));
        }
        return result;
    }

    private static List<String> wrap(String value, PDType0Font font, float width, float size) throws IOException {
        var words = value.split("\\s+");
        var lines = new ArrayList<String>();
        var current = new StringBuilder();
        for (var word : words) {
            var candidate = current.isEmpty() ? word : current + " " + word;
            if (!current.isEmpty() && font.getStringWidth(candidate) / 1000 * size > width) {
                lines.add(current.toString());
                current = new StringBuilder(word);
            } else {
                current = new StringBuilder(candidate);
            }
        }
        if (!current.isEmpty()) {
            lines.add(current.toString());
        }
        return lines.isEmpty() ? List.of("") : lines;
    }

    private static String fit(String value, PDType0Font font, float size, float width) throws IOException {
        var result = value;
        while (!result.isEmpty() && font.getStringWidth(result) / 1000 * size > width) {
            result = result.substring(0, result.length() - 1);
        }
        return result.equals(value) ? result : result + "…";
    }

    private static void write(PDPageContentStream stream, PDType0Font font, String value, float x, float y, float size, Color color) throws IOException {
        stream.beginText();
        stream.setNonStrokingColor(color);
        stream.setFont(font, size);
        stream.newLineAtOffset(x, y);
        stream.showText(value);
        stream.endText();
    }

    private static Path findFont() throws IOException {
        var candidates = List.of(
            Path.of("C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf"),
            Path.of("C:/Windows/Fonts/segoeui.ttf"),
            Path.of("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf"),
            Path.of("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation2/LiberationSans-Regular.ttf"),
            Path.of("/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf")
        );
        return candidates.stream().filter(Files::isRegularFile).findFirst().orElseThrow(() -> new IOException("A font with Ukrainian glyphs was not found on this computer."));
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\infrastructure\CommandRunner.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

public final class CommandRunner {
    private final Path repositoryRoot;

    public CommandRunner(Path repositoryRoot) {
        this.repositoryRoot = repositoryRoot;
    }

    public void run(String commandName) throws IOException, InterruptedException {
        var windows = System.getProperty("os.name").toLowerCase().contains("win");
        var commandFile = repositoryRoot.resolve("commands").resolve(windows ? "windows" : "linux").resolve(commandName + (windows ? ".ps1" : ".sh"));
        if (!java.nio.file.Files.isRegularFile(commandFile)) {
            throw new IOException("Command file not found: " + commandFile);
        }
        var builder = windows
            ? new ProcessBuilder("powershell.exe", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-File", commandFile.toString())
            : new ProcessBuilder("bash", commandFile.toString());
        builder.directory(repositoryRoot.toFile());
        builder.redirectErrorStream(true);
        var process = builder.start();
        var output = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        var reader = Thread.ofVirtual().start(() -> {
            try {
                process.getInputStream().transferTo(output);
            } catch (IOException ignored) {
            }
        });
        if (!process.waitFor(Duration.ofMinutes(2).toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)) {
            process.destroyForcibly();
            throw new IOException("Database command timed out: " + commandName);
        }
        reader.join();
        if (process.exitValue() != 0) {
            throw new IOException("Database command failed: " + commandName + System.lineSeparator() + output.toString(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\infrastructure\DatabaseBootstrapper.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure;

import ua.zunu.reports.config.AppConfig;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.sql.SQLException;

public final class DatabaseBootstrapper {
    private final AppConfig config;
    private final DatabaseConnectionFactory connections;
    private final CommandRunner commands;
    private final PasswordHashVerifier passwordHashes;

    public DatabaseBootstrapper(AppConfig config, DatabaseConnectionFactory connections, CommandRunner commands, PasswordHashVerifier passwordHashes) {
        this.config = config;
        this.connections = connections;
        this.commands = commands;
        this.passwordHashes = passwordHashes;
    }

    public void initialize() throws Exception {
        try {
            connections.open().close();
        } catch (SQLException exception) {
            if (!isInitializationError(exception)) {
                throw exception;
            }
            commands.run("create-database");
        }
        if (!schemaExists()) {
            commands.run("create-database");
        }
        if (!hasApplicationPrivileges()) {
            commands.run("create-database");
            if (!hasApplicationPrivileges()) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("The application database user still lacks table access after initialization.");
            }
        }
        if (!hasData()) {
            commands.run("populate-database");
        }
        ensureSeededDirectorPassword();
        try (var connection = connections.open(); var statement = connection.createStatement()) {
            statement.executeQuery("SELECT 1");
        }
    }

    private boolean schemaExists() throws SQLException {
        var sql = "SELECT to_regclass('public.customer') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.employee') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.car') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.orders') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.service') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.part') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.order_service') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.order_part') IS NOT NULL";
        try (var connection = connections.open(); var statement = connection.createStatement(); var result = statement.executeQuery(sql)) {
            return result.next() && result.getBoolean(1);
        }
    }

    private boolean hasApplicationPrivileges() throws SQLException {
        try (var connection = connections.open(); var statement = connection.createStatement(); var result = statement.executeQuery("SELECT has_table_privilege(current_user, 'customer', 'SELECT')")) {
            return result.next() && result.getBoolean(1);
        } catch (SQLException exception) {
            if ("42501".equals(exception.getSQLState())) {
                return false;
            }
            throw exception;
        }
    }

    private boolean hasData() throws SQLException {
        var sql = "SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM customer UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM employee UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM car UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM orders UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM service UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM part UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM order_service UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM order_part)";
        try (var connection = connections.open(); var statement = connection.createStatement(); var result = statement.executeQuery(sql)) {
            return result.next() && result.getBoolean(1);
        }
    }

    private void ensureSeededDirectorPassword() throws SQLException {
        var currentHash = "";
        try (var connection = connections.open(); var statement = connection.prepareStatement("SELECT password_hash FROM employee WHERE login = 'demo.director' AND role = 'director'")) {
            try (var result = statement.executeQuery()) {
                if (!result.next()) {
                    return;
                }
                currentHash = result.getString(1);
            }
        }
        if (!"!".equals(currentHash)) {
            return;
        }
        if (config.demoDirectorPassword().isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Set DEMO_DIRECTOR_PASSWORD in the shared .env file to enable the seeded director account.");
        }
        try (var connection = connections.open(); var statement = connection.prepareStatement("UPDATE employee SET password_hash = ? WHERE login = 'demo.director' AND role = 'director' AND password_hash = '!'")) {
            statement.setString(1, passwordHashes.hash(config.demoDirectorPassword()));
            statement.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    private static boolean isInitializationError(SQLException exception) {
        for (var current = exception; current != null; current = current.getNextException()) {
            if ("3D000".equals(current.getSQLState()) || "28P01".equals(current.getSQLState())) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\infrastructure\DatabaseConnectionFactory.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure;

import ua.zunu.reports.config.AppConfig;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.DriverManager;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Properties;

public final class DatabaseConnectionFactory {
    private final AppConfig config;

    public DatabaseConnectionFactory(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    public Connection open() throws SQLException {
        var url = "jdbc:postgresql://" + config.host() + ":" + config.port() + "/" + config.database() + "?connectTimeout=5&socketTimeout=15&ApplicationName=ServiceCenterReports";
        var properties = new Properties();
        properties.setProperty("user", config.appUser());
        properties.setProperty("password", config.appPassword());
        return DriverManager.getConnection(url, properties);
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\infrastructure\PasswordHashVerifier.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure;

import javax.crypto.SecretKeyFactory;
import javax.crypto.spec.PBEKeySpec;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Base64;

public final class PasswordHashVerifier {
    private static final int ITERATIONS = 100_000;
    private static final SecureRandom RANDOM = new SecureRandom();

    public boolean verify(String password, String encodedHash) {
        try {
            var decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encodedHash);
            if (decoded.length < 17 || decoded[0] != 1) {
                return false;
            }
            var header = ByteBuffer.wrap(decoded, 1, 12).order(ByteOrder.BIG_ENDIAN);
            var prf = header.getInt();
            var iterations = header.getInt();
            var saltLength = header.getInt();
            var subkeyOffset = 13 + saltLength;
            var subkeyLength = decoded.length - subkeyOffset;
            if (iterations < 1 || iterations > 1_000_000 || saltLength < 16 || saltLength > 1024 || subkeyLength < 16 || subkeyLength > 128) {
                return false;
            }
            var algorithm = switch (prf) {
                case 0 -> "PBKDF2WithHmacSHA1";
                case 1 -> "PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256";
                case 2 -> "PBKDF2WithHmacSHA512";
                default -> null;
            };
            if (algorithm == null) {
                return false;
            }
            var salt = Arrays.copyOfRange(decoded, 13, subkeyOffset);
            var expected = Arrays.copyOfRange(decoded, subkeyOffset, decoded.length);
            var actual = derive(password, salt, iterations, expected.length * 8, algorithm);
            return MessageDigest.isEqual(expected, actual);
        } catch (RuntimeException | java.security.GeneralSecurityException exception) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public String hash(String password) {
        var salt = new byte[16];
        RANDOM.nextBytes(salt);
        byte[] subkey;
        try {
            subkey = derive(password, salt, ITERATIONS, 256, "PBKDF2WithHmacSHA512");
        } catch (java.security.GeneralSecurityException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("The password hashing algorithm is not available.", exception);
        }
        var buffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(13 + salt.length + subkey.length).order(ByteOrder.BIG_ENDIAN);
        buffer.put((byte) 1);
        buffer.putInt(2);
        buffer.putInt(ITERATIONS);
        buffer.putInt(salt.length);
        buffer.put(salt);
        buffer.put(subkey);
        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(buffer.array());
    }

    private static byte[] derive(String password, byte[] salt, int iterations, int keyLength, String algorithm) throws java.security.GeneralSecurityException {
        var spec = new PBEKeySpec(password.toCharArray(), salt, iterations, keyLength);
        try {
            return SecretKeyFactory.getInstance(algorithm).generateSecret(spec).getEncoded();
        } finally {
            spec.clearPassword();
        }
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\ReportApplication.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports;

import javafx.application.Application;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import ua.zunu.reports.config.AppConfig;
import ua.zunu.reports.data.JdbcDirectorRepository;
import ua.zunu.reports.data.JdbcReportRepository;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.Director;
import ua.zunu.reports.export.ReportPdfExporter;
import ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure.CommandRunner;
import ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure.DatabaseBootstrapper;
import ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure.DatabaseConnectionFactory;
import ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure.PasswordHashVerifier;
import ua.zunu.reports.service.DirectorAuthenticationService;
import ua.zunu.reports.service.ReportService;
import ua.zunu.reports.ui.DashboardView;
import ua.zunu.reports.ui.LoginView;

public final class ReportApplication extends Application {
    private static DirectorAuthenticationService authentication;
    private ReportService reports;
    private ReportPdfExporter pdfExporter;

    @Override
    public void start(Stage stage) {
        try {
            var config = AppConfig.load();
            var connections = new DatabaseConnectionFactory(config);
            var commands = new CommandRunner(config.repositoryRoot());
            var hashes = new PasswordHashVerifier();
            new DatabaseBootstrapper(config, connections, commands, hashes).initialize();
            authentication = new DirectorAuthenticationService(new JdbcDirectorRepository(connections), hashes);
            reports = new ReportService(new JdbcReportRepository(connections));
            pdfExporter = new ReportPdfExporter();
            new LoginView(authentication).show(stage, director -> openDashboard(stage, director));
        } catch (Exception exception) {
            var alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR);
            alert.setTitle("GarageFlow · Помилка запуску");
            alert.setHeaderText("Не вдалося підготувати застосунок звітів");
            alert.setContentText(exception.getMessage());
            alert.showAndWait();
            stage.close();
        }
    }

    private void openDashboard(Stage stage, Director director) {
        new DashboardView(reports, pdfExporter).show(stage, director);
    }

    public static DirectorAuthenticationService authentication() {
        return authentication;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        launch(args);
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\service\DirectorAuthenticationService.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.service;

import ua.zunu.reports.data.DirectorRepository;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.Director;
import ua.zunu.reports.infrastructure.PasswordHashVerifier;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Optional;

public final class DirectorAuthenticationService {
    private final DirectorRepository directors;
    private final PasswordHashVerifier passwordHashes;

    public DirectorAuthenticationService(DirectorRepository directors, PasswordHashVerifier passwordHashes) {
        this.directors = directors;
        this.passwordHashes = passwordHashes;
    }

    public Optional<Director> authenticate(String login, String password) throws SQLException {
        if (login == null || login.isBlank() || password == null || password.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Введіть логін і пароль.");
        }
        var director = directors.findByLogin(login.strip());
        if (director.isEmpty() || !"director".equals(director.get().role()) || !passwordHashes.verify(password, director.get().passwordHash())) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        return director;
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\service\ReportService.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.service;

import ua.zunu.reports.data.ReportRepository;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.DateRange;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportResult;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportType;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.LocalDate;

public final class ReportService {
    private final ReportRepository reports;

    public ReportService(ReportRepository reports) {
        this.reports = reports;
    }

    public ReportResult load(ReportType type, LocalDate from, LocalDate to) throws SQLException {
        if (type == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Оберіть тип звіту.");
        }
        return reports.load(type, new DateRange(from, to));
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\ui\DashboardView.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.ui;

import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.beans.property.ReadOnlyStringWrapper;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.chart.BarChart;
import javafx.scene.chart.CategoryAxis;
import javafx.scene.chart.LineChart;
import javafx.scene.chart.NumberAxis;
import javafx.scene.chart.XYChart;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.DatePicker;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ScrollPane;
import javafx.scene.control.TableColumn;
import javafx.scene.control.TableView;
import javafx.scene.control.Tooltip;
import javafx.scene.layout.BorderPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import ua.zunu.reports.ReportApplication;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.Director;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportResult;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.ReportType;
import ua.zunu.reports.export.ReportPdfExporter;
import ua.zunu.reports.service.ReportService;

import java.awt.Desktop;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public final class DashboardView {
    private final ReportService reports;
    private final ReportPdfExporter pdfExporter;
    private final ExecutorService worker = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(Thread.ofPlatform().daemon().factory());
    private final VBox navigation = new VBox(7);
    private final VBox chartBox = new VBox(10);
    private final TableView<java.util.List<String>> table = new TableView<>();
    private final Label title = new Label();
    private final Label description = new Label();
    private final Label rowCount = new Label();
    private final Label status = new Label();
    private final DatePicker fromDate = new DatePicker(LocalDate.now().withDayOfYear(1));
    private final DatePicker toDate = new DatePicker(LocalDate.now());
    private final Button exportButton = new Button("↓  Експорт PDF");
    private ReportType selected = ReportType.ORDERS;
    private ReportResult currentReport;

    public DashboardView(ReportService reports, ReportPdfExporter pdfExporter) {
        this.reports = reports;
        this.pdfExporter = pdfExporter;
    }

    public void show(Stage stage, Director director) {
        var shell = new BorderPane();
        shell.getStyleClass().add("app-shell");
        shell.setLeft(buildSidebar(stage, director));
        shell.setCenter(buildContent(director));
        var scene = new Scene(shell, 1440, 900);
        scene.getStylesheets().add(getClass().getResource("/ua/zunu/reports/ui/theme.css").toExternalForm());
        stage.setTitle("GarageFlow · Аналітика");
        stage.setMinWidth(1120);
        stage.setMinHeight(720);
        stage.setScene(scene);
        stage.centerOnScreen();
        stage.show();
        stage.setOnCloseRequest(event -> worker.shutdownNow());
        selectReport(selected);
    }

    private VBox buildSidebar(Stage stage, Director director) {
        var sidebar = new VBox(18);
        sidebar.getStyleClass().add("sidebar");
        sidebar.setPadding(new Insets(28, 18, 18, 18));
        sidebar.setPrefWidth(275);
        var brand = new HBox(12, new Label("G"), new VBox(1, new Label("garageflow"), new Label("ЗВІТИ СЕРВІСУ")));
        brand.getStyleClass().add("brand-block");
        brand.getChildren().getFirst().getStyleClass().add("brand-mark");
        brand.getChildren().get(1).getStyleClass().add("brand-copy");
        var section = new Label("АНАЛІТИКА");
        section.getStyleClass().add("side-caption");
        navigation.getStyleClass().add("report-navigation");
        for (var report : ReportType.values()) {
            var button = new Button(report.title());
            button.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
            button.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
            button.getStyleClass().add("report-nav-button");
            button.setOnAction(event -> selectReport(report));
            navigation.getChildren().add(button);
        }
        var spacer = new VBox();
        VBox.setVgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
        var directorLabel = new Label(director.firstName() + " " + director.lastName());
        directorLabel.getStyleClass().add("director-name");
        var roleLabel = new Label("КЕРІВНИК");
        roleLabel.getStyleClass().add("director-role");
        var userCard = new VBox(4, directorLabel, roleLabel);
        userCard.getStyleClass().add("user-card");
        var logout = new Button("↪  Вийти із системи");
        logout.getStyleClass().add("logout-button");
        logout.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        logout.setOnAction(event -> {
            worker.shutdownNow();
            new LoginView(ReportApplication.authentication()).show(stage, next -> new DashboardView(reports, pdfExporter).show(stage, next));
        });
        sidebar.getChildren().addAll(brand, section, navigation, spacer, userCard, logout);
        return sidebar;
    }

    private VBox buildContent(Director director) {
        var content = new VBox(22);
        content.getStyleClass().add("main-content");
        content.setPadding(new Insets(34, 42, 36, 42));
        var eyebrow = new Label("ЦЕНТР КЕРУВАННЯ  /  " + director.login().toUpperCase());
        eyebrow.getStyleClass().add("eyebrow");
        title.getStyleClass().add("page-title");
        description.getStyleClass().add("page-description");
        var heading = new VBox(7, eyebrow, title, description);
        var startLabel = new Label("Від");
        var endLabel = new Label("До");
        startLabel.getStyleClass().add("field-label");
        endLabel.getStyleClass().add("field-label");
        fromDate.getStyleClass().add("date-input");
        toDate.getStyleClass().add("date-input");
        var apply = new Button("Оновити звіт");
        apply.getStyleClass().addAll("button", "button-primary");
        apply.setOnAction(event -> loadSelectedReport());
        exportButton.getStyleClass().addAll("button", "button-secondary");
        exportButton.setDisable(true);
        exportButton.setOnAction(event -> exportCurrentReport());
        var filters = new HBox(12, field(startLabel, fromDate), field(endLabel, toDate), apply, exportButton);
        filters.setAlignment(Pos.BOTTOM_LEFT);
        filters.getStyleClass().add("filter-panel");
        var chartHeader = new HBox(new Label("ДИНАМІКА ПОКАЗНИКІВ"));
        chartHeader.getStyleClass().add("chart-heading");
        chartBox.getStyleClass().add("chart-card");
        chartBox.getChildren().addAll(chartHeader, new Label("Оберіть звіт для побудови графіка."));
        var tableHeading = new HBox(10, new Label("ДЕТАЛІЗАЦІЯ"), rowCount);
        tableHeading.getStyleClass().add("table-heading");
        table.getStyleClass().add("report-table");
        table.setPlaceholder(new Label("Немає записів за обраний період"));
        VBox.setVgrow(table, Priority.ALWAYS);
        status.getStyleClass().add("status-message");
        var scroll = new ScrollPane(new VBox(22, heading, filters, chartBox, tableHeading, table, status));
        scroll.setFitToWidth(true);
        scroll.getStyleClass().add("content-scroll");
        VBox.setVgrow(scroll, Priority.ALWAYS);
        content.getChildren().add(scroll);
        return content;
    }

    private void selectReport(ReportType report) {
        selected = report;
        var index = 0;
        for (var node : navigation.getChildren()) {
            node.getStyleClass().remove("active");
            if (index++ == report.ordinal()) {
                node.getStyleClass().add("active");
            }
        }
        loadSelectedReport();
    }

    private void loadSelectedReport() {
        try {
            selectedRange();
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {
            showError(exception.getMessage());
            return;
        }
        title.setText(selected.title());
        description.setText(selected.description());
        status.setText("Формуємо звіт…");
        exportButton.setDisable(true);
        var type = selected;
        var from = fromDate.getValue();
        var to = toDate.getValue();
        CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            try {
                return reports.load(type, from, to);
            } catch (SQLException exception) {
                throw new RuntimeException(exception);
            }
        }, worker).whenComplete((report, error) -> Platform.runLater(() -> {
            if (error != null) {
                status.setText("Не вдалося отримати дані з бази.");
                showError("Не вдалося сформувати звіт. Перевір підключення до PostgreSQL.");
                return;
            }
            currentReport = report;
            renderTable(report);
            renderChart(report);
            rowCount.setText(report.rows().size() + " записів");
            status.setText("Оновлено " + LocalDate.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd.MM.yyyy")));
            exportButton.setDisable(false);
        }));
    }

    private void renderTable(ReportResult report) {
        table.getColumns().clear();
        table.getItems().clear();
        for (var index = 0; index < report.columns().size(); index++) {
            var columnIndex = index;
            var column = new TableColumn<java.util.List<String>, String>(report.columns().get(index));
            column.setCellValueFactory(cell -> new ReadOnlyStringWrapper(cell.getValue().get(columnIndex)));
            column.setPrefWidth(Math.max(115, 850.0 / report.columns().size()));
            column.setMinWidth(90);
            table.getColumns().add(column);
        }
        table.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(report.rows()));
    }

    private void renderChart(ReportResult report) {
        chartBox.getChildren().removeIf(node -> node instanceof javafx.scene.chart.Chart || node.getStyleClass().contains("chart-empty"));
        if (report.type().chartType() == ua.zunu.reports.domain.ChartType.NONE) {
            chartBox.getChildren().add(new Label("Цей звіт зручно переглядати у таблиці."));
            chartBox.getChildren().getLast().getStyleClass().add("chart-empty");
            return;
        }
        if (report.chartPoints().isEmpty()) {
            chartBox.getChildren().add(new Label("За обраний період немає даних для графіка."));
            chartBox.getChildren().getLast().getStyleClass().add("chart-empty");
            return;
        }
        var xAxis = new CategoryAxis();
        xAxis.setTickLabelRotation(20);
        var yAxis = new NumberAxis();
        yAxis.setForceZeroInRange(true);
        var chart = report.type().chartType() == ua.zunu.reports.domain.ChartType.LINE
            ? new LineChart<String, Number>(xAxis, yAxis)
            : new BarChart<String, Number>(xAxis, yAxis);
        chart.setLegendVisible(false);
        chart.setAnimated(false);
        chart.setTitle(report.chartSeriesName());
        if (chart instanceof LineChart<String, Number> lineChart) {
            lineChart.setCreateSymbols(true);
        }
        var series = new XYChart.Series<String, Number>();
        series.setName(report.chartSeriesName());
        report.chartPoints().stream().limit(16).forEach(point -> series.getData().add(new XYChart.Data<>(point.label(), point.value())));
        chart.getData().add(series);
        chart.setPrefHeight(280);
        chartBox.getChildren().add(chart);
    }

    private void exportCurrentReport() {
        if (currentReport == null) {
            return;
        }
        var chooser = new FileChooser();
        chooser.setTitle("Зберегти звіт у PDF");
        chooser.setInitialFileName("garageflow-" + selected.name().toLowerCase() + ".pdf");
        chooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("PDF document", "*.pdf"));
        var selectedFile = chooser.showSaveDialog(table.getScene().getWindow());
        if (selectedFile == null) {
            return;
        }
        var file = selectedFile.getName().toLowerCase().endsWith(".pdf")
            ? selectedFile
            : new java.io.File(selectedFile.getParentFile(), selectedFile.getName() + ".pdf");
        status.setText("Створюємо PDF…");
        exportButton.setDisable(true);
        var report = currentReport;
        CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
            try {
                pdfExporter.export(report, Path.of(file.toURI()));
                if (Desktop.isDesktopSupported()) {
                    Desktop.getDesktop().open(file);
                }
            } catch (IOException exception) {
                throw new RuntimeException(exception);
            }
        }, worker).whenComplete((ignored, error) -> Platform.runLater(() -> {
            exportButton.setDisable(false);
            if (error != null) {
                status.setText("Не вдалося створити PDF.");
                showError("Не вдалося експортувати PDF. Перевір обрану папку та наявність шрифту з українськими літерами.");
                return;
            }
            status.setText("PDF збережено: " + file.getAbsolutePath());
        }));
    }

    private javafx.scene.Node field(Label label, DatePicker picker) {
        var box = new VBox(6, label, picker);
        box.getStyleClass().add("date-field");
        return box;
    }

    private ua.zunu.reports.domain.DateRange selectedRange() {
        return new ua.zunu.reports.domain.DateRange(fromDate.getValue(), toDate.getValue());
    }

    private static void showError(String message) {
        var alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, message);
        alert.setHeaderText("Потрібна перевірка");
        alert.setTitle("GarageFlow · Повідомлення");
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: JavaReports\src\main\java\ua\zunu\reports\ui\LoginView.java =====
package ua.zunu.reports.ui;

import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.PasswordField;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import javafx.scene.layout.BorderPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.StackPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import ua.zunu.reports.domain.Director;
import ua.zunu.reports.service.DirectorAuthenticationService;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

public final class LoginView {
    private final DirectorAuthenticationService authentication;

    public LoginView(DirectorAuthenticationService authentication) {
        this.authentication = authentication;
    }

    public void show(Stage stage, Consumer<Director> onAuthenticated) {
        var root = new BorderPane();
        root.getStyleClass().add("login-page");

        var brand = new Label("GARAGEFLOW");
        brand.getStyleClass().add("login-brand");
        var brandSubhead = new Label("SERVICE CENTER  /  MANAGEMENT");
        brandSubhead.getStyleClass().add("login-brand-subhead");
        var top = new VBox(5, brand, brandSubhead);
        top.setPadding(new Insets(38, 48, 20, 48));
        root.setTop(top);

        var art = new VBox(20);
        art.getStyleClass().add("login-art");
        art.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        art.setPadding(new Insets(40, 64, 50, 64));
        var artEyebrow = new Label("ЗВІТНІСТЬ СЕРВІСНОГО ЦЕНТРУ");
        artEyebrow.getStyleClass().add("eyebrow-light");
        var artTitle = new Label("Бачити більше.\nКерувати точніше.");
        artTitle.getStyleClass().add("login-art-title");
        var artText = new Label("Аналітика роботи, послуг і клієнтів в одному просторі.");
        artText.getStyleClass().add("login-art-text");
        art.getChildren().addAll(artEyebrow, artTitle, artText);
        root.setLeft(art);

        var login = new TextField();
        login.setPromptText("Логін керівника");
        login.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        login.getStyleClass().add("input");
        var password = new PasswordField();
        password.setPromptText("Пароль");
        password.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        password.getStyleClass().add("input");
        var error = new Label();
        error.getStyleClass().add("login-error");
        error.setWrapText(true);
        var button = new Button("Увійти до звітів  →");
        button.getStyleClass().addAll("button", "button-primary");
        button.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);

        var cardTitle = new Label("Вхід до системи");
        cardTitle.getStyleClass().add("login-card-title");
        var cardText = new Label("Доступ надається лише обліковому запису керівника.");
        cardText.getStyleClass().add("muted-text");
        var form = new VBox(13, cardTitle, cardText, fieldLabel("Логін", login), fieldLabel("Пароль", password), error, button);
        form.getStyleClass().add("login-form");
        form.setMaxWidth(390);
        form.setPadding(new Insets(38));
        StackPane card = new StackPane(form);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(32, 48, 48, 48));
        root.setCenter(card);

        var signIn = (Runnable) () -> {
            try {
                var director = authentication.authenticate(login.getText(), password.getText());
                if (director.isEmpty()) {
                    error.setText("Не вдалося увійти. Перевір логін і пароль керівника.");
                    return;
                }
                onAuthenticated.accept(director.get());
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {
                error.setText(exception.getMessage());
            } catch (SQLException exception) {
                error.setText("Не вдалося перевірити обліковий запис у базі даних.");
            }
        };
        button.setOnAction(event -> signIn.run());
        password.setOnAction(event -> signIn.run());

        var scene = new Scene(root, 1180, 760);
        scene.getStylesheets().add(getClass().getResource("/ua/zunu/reports/ui/theme.css").toExternalForm());
        stage.setTitle("GarageFlow · Звіти");
        stage.setMinWidth(980);
        stage.setMinHeight(680);
        stage.setScene(scene);
        stage.centerOnScreen();
        stage.show();
    }

    private static VBox fieldLabel(String title, TextField field) {
        var label = new Label(title);
        label.getStyleClass().add("field-label");
        return new VBox(7, label, field);
    }
}
