===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Controllers\AccountController.cs =====
using System.Security.Claims;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.Cookies;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Controllers;

public sealed class AccountController(
    IEmployeeRepository employees,
    IPasswordHasher<Employee> passwordHasher) : Controller
{
    [AllowAnonymous]
    [HttpGet]
    public IActionResult Login(string? returnUrl = null)
    {
        if (User.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true)
        {
            return RedirectToAction("Index", "Home");
        }

        ViewBag.ReturnUrl = returnUrl;
        return View(new LoginForm());
    }

    [AllowAnonymous]
    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Login(LoginForm form, string? returnUrl = null, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ViewBag.ReturnUrl = returnUrl;
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(form);
        }

        var employee = await employees.FindByLoginAsync(form.Login.Trim(), cancellationToken);
        if (employee is null || passwordHasher.VerifyHashedPassword(employee, employee.PasswordHash, form.Password) == PasswordVerificationResult.Failed)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty, "Неправильний логін або пароль.");
            return View(form);
        }

        var claims = new List<Claim>
        {
            new(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier, employee.EmployeeId.ToString()),
            new(ClaimTypes.Name, employee.Login),
            new(ClaimTypes.GivenName, employee.FirstName),
            new(ClaimTypes.Surname, employee.LastName),
            new(ClaimTypes.Role, employee.Role)
        };
        var identity = new ClaimsIdentity(claims, CookieAuthenticationDefaults.AuthenticationScheme);
        await HttpContext.SignInAsync(CookieAuthenticationDefaults.AuthenticationScheme, new ClaimsPrincipal(identity));

        return Url.IsLocalUrl(returnUrl) ? LocalRedirect(returnUrl!) : RedirectToAction("Index", "Home");
    }

    [Authorize]
    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Logout()
    {
        await HttpContext.SignOutAsync(CookieAuthenticationDefaults.AuthenticationScheme);
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Login));
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Controllers\CarsController.cs =====
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;
using VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Controllers;

[Authorize(Roles = AccessRoles.Staff)]
public sealed class CarsController(ICarRepository cars, FormOptionsService options) : DataErrorController
{
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        return View(await cars.GetAllAsync(cancellationToken));
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await LoadCustomersAsync(cancellationToken);
        return View(new CarForm());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(CarForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await LoadCustomersAsync(cancellationToken);
            return View(form);
        }

        try
        {
            await cars.CreateAsync(form, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Автомобіль зареєстровано.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            await LoadCustomersAsync(cancellationToken);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var form = await cars.GetFormAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        if (form is null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        await LoadCustomersAsync(cancellationToken);
        return View(form);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(CarForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await LoadCustomersAsync(cancellationToken);
            return View(form);
        }

        try
        {
            await cars.UpdateAsync(form, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Дані автомобіля оновлено.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            await LoadCustomersAsync(cancellationToken);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        try
        {
            await cars.DeleteAsync(id, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Автомобіль видалено.";
        }
        catch (Exception)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "Автомобіль має історію замовлень і не може бути видалений.";
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    private async Task LoadCustomersAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        ViewBag.Customers = await options.CustomersAsync(cancellationToken);
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Controllers\CustomersController.cs =====
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;
using VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Controllers;

[Authorize(Roles = AccessRoles.Staff)]
public sealed class CustomersController(ICustomerRepository customers) : DataErrorController
{
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        return View(await customers.GetAllAsync(cancellationToken));
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public IActionResult Create()
    {
        return View(new CustomerForm());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(CustomerForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(form);
        }

        try
        {
            await customers.CreateAsync(form, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Клієнта додано.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var form = await customers.GetFormAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        return form is null ? NotFound() : View(form);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(CustomerForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(form);
        }

        try
        {
            await customers.UpdateAsync(form, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Дані клієнта оновлено.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        try
        {
            await customers.DeleteAsync(id, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Клієнта видалено.";
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = exception is Npgsql.PostgresException { SqlState: Npgsql.PostgresErrorCodes.ForeignKeyViolation }
                ? "У клієнта є зареєстровані авто. Спочатку видаліть їх або змініть власника."
                : "Не вдалося видалити клієнта.";
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Controllers\EmployeesController.cs =====
using System.Security.Claims;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;
using VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Controllers;

[Authorize(Roles = AccessRoles.Admin)]
public sealed class EmployeesController(
    IEmployeeRepository employees,
    IPasswordHasher<Employee> passwordHasher) : DataErrorController
{
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        return View(await employees.GetAllAsync(cancellationToken));
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public IActionResult Create()
    {
        return View(new EmployeeCreateForm());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(EmployeeCreateForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(form);
        }

        var employee = new Employee
        {
            FirstName = form.FirstName.Trim(),
            LastName = form.LastName.Trim(),
            Role = form.Role,
            Login = form.Login.Trim()
        };
        employee.PasswordHash = passwordHasher.HashPassword(employee, form.Password);

        try
        {
            await employees.CreateAsync(employee, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Обліковий запис створено.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var employee = await employees.GetAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        if (employee is null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(new EmployeeEditForm
        {
            EmployeeId = employee.EmployeeId,
            FirstName = employee.FirstName,
            LastName = employee.LastName,
            Role = employee.Role,
            Login = employee.Login
        });
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(EmployeeEditForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(form);
        }

        var current = await employees.GetAsync(form.EmployeeId, cancellationToken);
        if (current is null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var employee = new Employee
        {
            EmployeeId = form.EmployeeId,
            FirstName = form.FirstName.Trim(),
            LastName = form.LastName.Trim(),
            Role = form.Role,
            Login = form.Login.Trim(),
            PasswordHash = current.PasswordHash
        };
        var passwordHash = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(form.Password)
            ? null
            : passwordHasher.HashPassword(employee, form.Password);

        try
        {
            await employees.UpdateAsync(employee, passwordHash, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Обліковий запис оновлено.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (int.TryParse(User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier), out var currentId) && currentId == id)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "Не можна видалити власний обліковий запис під час активного сеансу.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }

        try
        {
            await employees.DeleteAsync(id, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Обліковий запис видалено.";
        }
        catch (Exception)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "Працівник використовується в замовленнях і не може бути видалений.";
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Controllers\HistoryController.cs =====
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;
using VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Controllers;

[Authorize(Roles = AccessRoles.Staff + "," + AccessRoles.Guest)]
public sealed class HistoryController(ICarRepository cars, IOrderRepository orders) : Controller
{
    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index(HistoryLookupForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!Request.Query.ContainsKey(nameof(form.CarId)))
        {
            return View(new HistoryPage());
        }

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(new HistoryPage { CarId = form.CarId });
        }

        var car = await cars.GetAsync(form.CarId, cancellationToken);
        if (car is null)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(form.CarId), "Автомобіль із таким системним ідентифікатором не знайдено.");
            return View(new HistoryPage { CarId = form.CarId });
        }

        return View(new HistoryPage
        {
            CarId = form.CarId,
            Car = car,
            Orders = await orders.GetCarHistoryAsync(form.CarId, cancellationToken)
        });
    }
}

===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Controllers\OrdersController.cs =====
using System.Security.Claims;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;
using VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Controllers;

[Authorize(Roles = AccessRoles.Staff)]
public sealed class OrdersController(
    IOrderRepository orders,
    FormOptionsService options) : DataErrorController
{
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index(string? query, string? status, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        ViewBag.Query = query;
        ViewBag.Status = status;
        return View(await orders.GetAllAsync(query, status, cancellationToken));
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await LoadCarsAsync(cancellationToken);
        return View(new OrderCreateForm());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(OrderCreateForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await LoadCarsAsync(cancellationToken);
            return View(form);
        }

        if (!int.TryParse(User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier), out var employeeId))
        {
            return Forbid();
        }

        try
        {
            var orderId = await orders.CreateAsync(form, employeeId, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Замовлення створено.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id = orderId });
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            await LoadCarsAsync(cancellationToken);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var details = await orders.GetDetailsAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        if (details is null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        await LoadDetailOptionsAsync(cancellationToken);
        return View(details);
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var details = await orders.GetDetailsAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        if (details is null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        await LoadCarsAsync(cancellationToken);
        return View(new OrderEditForm
        {
            OrderId = details.OrderId,
            CarId = details.CarId,
            OrderDate = details.OrderDate,
            Deadline = details.Deadline,
            Mileage = details.Mileage
        });
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(OrderEditForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await LoadCarsAsync(cancellationToken);
            return View(form);
        }

        try
        {
            await orders.UpdateAsync(form, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Замовлення оновлено.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id = form.OrderId });
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            await LoadCarsAsync(cancellationToken);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> AddService(int id, AddServiceForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return await InvalidDetailsAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        }

        try
        {
            await orders.AddServiceAsync(id, form, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Послугу додано до замовлення.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id });
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            return await InvalidDetailsAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        }
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> AddPart(int id, AddPartForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return await InvalidDetailsAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        }

        try
        {
            await orders.AddPartAsync(id, form, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Запчастину додано до замовлення.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id });
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            return await InvalidDetailsAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        }
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RemoveService(int id, int serviceId, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        return await ChangeLineAsync(id, () => orders.RemoveServiceAsync(id, serviceId, cancellationToken), cancellationToken);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RemovePart(int id, int partId, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        return await ChangeLineAsync(id, () => orders.RemovePartAsync(id, partId, cancellationToken), cancellationToken);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Discount(int id, DiscountForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return await InvalidDetailsAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        }

        try
        {
            await orders.UpdateDiscountAsync(id, form.Discount, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Знижку застосовано.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id });
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            return await InvalidDetailsAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        }
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Close(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        try
        {
            await orders.CloseAsync(id, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Замовлення закрито. Підсумкову вартість зафіксовано.";
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = exception.Message;
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id });
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        try
        {
            await orders.DeleteAsync(id, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Чернетку замовлення видалено.";
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = exception.Message;
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    private async Task<IActionResult> ChangeLineAsync(int id, Func<Task> change, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        try
        {
            await change();
            TempData["Notice"] = "Деталі замовлення оновлено.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id });
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = exception.Message;
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id });
        }
    }

    private async Task<IActionResult> InvalidDetailsAsync(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var details = await orders.GetDetailsAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        if (details is null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        await LoadDetailOptionsAsync(cancellationToken);
        return View(nameof(Details), details);
    }

    private async Task LoadCarsAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        ViewBag.Cars = await options.CarsAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private async Task LoadDetailOptionsAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        ViewBag.Services = await options.ServicesAsync(cancellationToken);
        ViewBag.Parts = await options.PartsAsync(cancellationToken);
        ViewBag.Employees = await options.EmployeesAsync(cancellationToken);
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Controllers\PartsController.cs =====

===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Controllers\ServicesController.cs =====
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;
using VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Controllers;

[Authorize(Roles = AccessRoles.CatalogManagers)]
public sealed class ServicesController(ICatalogRepository catalog) : DataErrorController
{
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        return View(await catalog.GetServicesAsync(cancellationToken));
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public IActionResult Create()
    {
        return View(new ServiceForm());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(ServiceForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(form);
        }

        try
        {
            await catalog.CreateServiceAsync(form, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Послугу додано.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var form = await catalog.GetServiceFormAsync(id, cancellationToken);
        return form is null ? NotFound() : View(form);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(ServiceForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(form);
        }

        try
        {
            await catalog.UpdateServiceAsync(form, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Послугу оновлено.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            AddDataError(exception);
            return View(form);
        }
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        try
        {
            await catalog.DeleteServiceAsync(id, cancellationToken);
            TempData["Notice"] = "Послугу видалено.";
        }
        catch (Exception)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "Послуга використовується в замовленнях і не може бути видалена.";
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Data\FormOptionsService.cs =====
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Rendering;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Data;

public sealed class FormOptionsService(
    ICustomerRepository customers,
    ICarRepository cars,
    IEmployeeRepository employees,
    ICatalogRepository catalog)
{
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<SelectListItem>> CustomersAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return (await customers.GetAllAsync(cancellationToken))
            .Select(item => new SelectListItem($"{item.LastName} {item.FirstName} · {item.Phone}", item.CustomerId.ToString()))
            .ToArray();
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<SelectListItem>> CarsAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return (await cars.GetAllAsync(cancellationToken))
            .Select(item => new SelectListItem($"{item.Model} · {item.LicensePlate} · {item.CustomerName}", item.CarId.ToString()))
            .ToArray();
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<SelectListItem>> EmployeesAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return (await employees.GetAllAsync(cancellationToken))
            .Where(item => item.Role is "manager" or "admin")
            .Select(item => new SelectListItem($"{item.LastName} {item.FirstName}", item.EmployeeId.ToString()))
            .ToArray();
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<SelectListItem>> ServicesAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return (await catalog.GetServicesAsync(cancellationToken))
            .Select(item => new SelectListItem($"{item.Name} · {item.Price:N2} ₴", item.Id.ToString()))
            .ToArray();
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<SelectListItem>> PartsAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return (await catalog.GetPartsAsync(cancellationToken))
            .Select(item => new SelectListItem($"{item.Name} · {item.Article} · {item.Price:N2} ₴", item.Id.ToString()))
            .ToArray();
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Data\Repositories\CarRepository.cs =====
using Npgsql;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;

public sealed class CarRepository(NpgsqlDataSource dataSource) : ICarRepository
{
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<Car>> GetAllAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT c.car_id, c.customer_id, c.vin, c.license_plate, c.model, concat_ws(' ', cu.last_name, cu.first_name) FROM car c JOIN customer cu ON cu.customer_id = c.customer_id ORDER BY c.model, c.license_plate";
        var cars = new List<Car>();
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        while (await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken))
        {
            cars.Add(ReadCar(reader));
        }

        return cars;
    }

    public async Task<Car?> GetAsync(int carId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT c.car_id, c.customer_id, c.vin, c.license_plate, c.model, concat_ws(' ', cu.last_name, cu.first_name) FROM car c JOIN customer cu ON cu.customer_id = c.customer_id WHERE c.car_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", carId);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        return await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken) ? ReadCar(reader) : null;
    }

    public async Task<CarForm?> GetFormAsync(int carId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT car_id, customer_id, vin, license_plate, model FROM car WHERE car_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", carId);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        return await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken)
            ? new CarForm
            {
                CarId = reader.GetInt32(0),
                CustomerId = reader.GetInt32(1),
                Vin = reader.GetString(2),
                LicensePlate = reader.GetString(3),
                Model = reader.GetString(4)
            }
            : null;
    }

    public async Task<int> CreateAsync(CarForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "INSERT INTO car (customer_id, vin, license_plate, model) VALUES (@customer, @vin, @plate, @model) RETURNING car_id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        AddParameters(command, form);
        return (int)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? 0);
    }

    public async Task UpdateAsync(CarForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "UPDATE car SET customer_id = @customer, vin = @vin, license_plate = @plate, model = @model WHERE car_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        AddParameters(command, form);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", form.CarId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task DeleteAsync(int carId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("DELETE FROM car WHERE car_id = @id", connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", carId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private static Car ReadCar(NpgsqlDataReader reader)
    {
        return new Car
        {
            CarId = reader.GetInt32(0),
            CustomerId = reader.GetInt32(1),
            Vin = reader.GetString(2),
            LicensePlate = reader.GetString(3),
            Model = reader.GetString(4),
            CustomerName = reader.GetString(5)
        };
    }

    private static void AddParameters(NpgsqlCommand command, CarForm form)
    {
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("customer", form.CustomerId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("vin", form.Vin.Trim().ToUpperInvariant());
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("plate", form.LicensePlate.Trim().ToUpperInvariant());
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("model", form.Model.Trim());
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Data\Repositories\CatalogRepository.cs =====
using Npgsql;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;

public sealed class CatalogRepository(NpgsqlDataSource dataSource) : ICatalogRepository
{
    public Task<IReadOnlyList<CatalogItem>> GetServicesAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return GetItemsAsync("service", "service_id", false, cancellationToken);
    }

    public Task<IReadOnlyList<CatalogItem>> GetPartsAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return GetItemsAsync("part", "part_id", true, cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task<ServiceForm?> GetServiceFormAsync(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT service_id, name, price FROM service WHERE service_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", id);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        return await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken)
            ? new ServiceForm { Id = reader.GetInt32(0), Name = reader.GetString(1), Price = reader.GetDecimal(2) }
            : null;
    }

    public async Task<int> CreateServiceAsync(ServiceForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("INSERT INTO service (name, price) VALUES (@name, @price) RETURNING service_id", connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("name", form.Name.Trim());
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("price", form.Price);
        return (int)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? 0);
    }

    public async Task UpdateServiceAsync(ServiceForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("UPDATE service SET name = @name, price = @price WHERE service_id = @id", connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("name", form.Name.Trim());
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("price", form.Price);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", form.Id);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public Task DeleteServiceAsync(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return DeleteAsync("service", "service_id", id, cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task<PartForm?> GetPartFormAsync(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT part_id, name, article, price FROM part WHERE part_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", id);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        return await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken)
            ? new PartForm { Id = reader.GetInt32(0), Name = reader.GetString(1), Article = reader.GetString(2), Price = reader.GetDecimal(3) }
            : null;
    }

    public async Task<int> CreatePartAsync(PartForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("INSERT INTO part (name, article, price) VALUES (@name, @article, @price) RETURNING part_id", connection);
        AddPartParameters(command, form);
        return (int)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? 0);
    }

    public async Task UpdatePartAsync(PartForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("UPDATE part SET name = @name, article = @article, price = @price WHERE part_id = @id", connection);
        AddPartParameters(command, form);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", form.Id);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public Task DeletePartAsync(int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return DeleteAsync("part", "part_id", id, cancellationToken);
    }

    private async Task<IReadOnlyList<CatalogItem>> GetItemsAsync(string table, string idColumn, bool isPart, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var article = isPart ? ", article" : string.Empty;
        var sql = $"SELECT {idColumn}, name{article}, price FROM {table} ORDER BY name";
        var items = new List<CatalogItem>();
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        while (await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken))
        {
            items.Add(new CatalogItem
            {
                Id = reader.GetInt32(0),
                Name = reader.GetString(1),
                Article = isPart ? reader.GetString(2) : string.Empty,
                Price = reader.GetDecimal(isPart ? 3 : 2),
                IsPart = isPart
            });
        }

        return items;
    }

    private async Task DeleteAsync(string table, string idColumn, int id, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand($"DELETE FROM {table} WHERE {idColumn} = @id", connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", id);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private static void AddPartParameters(NpgsqlCommand command, PartForm form)
    {
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("name", form.Name.Trim());
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("article", form.Article.Trim().ToUpperInvariant());
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("price", form.Price);
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Data\Repositories\CustomerRepository.cs =====
using Npgsql;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;

public sealed class CustomerRepository(NpgsqlDataSource dataSource) : ICustomerRepository
{
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<Customer>> GetAllAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT cu.customer_id, cu.customer_type, cu.first_name, cu.last_name, cu.phone, COUNT(c.car_id)::int FROM customer cu LEFT JOIN car c ON c.customer_id = cu.customer_id GROUP BY cu.customer_id ORDER BY cu.last_name, cu.first_name";
        var customers = new List<Customer>();
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        while (await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken))
        {
            customers.Add(new Customer
            {
                CustomerId = reader.GetInt32(0),
                CustomerType = reader.GetString(1),
                FirstName = reader.GetString(2),
                LastName = reader.GetString(3),
                Phone = reader.GetString(4),
                CarCount = reader.GetInt32(5)
            });
        }

        return customers;
    }

    public async Task<CustomerForm?> GetFormAsync(int customerId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT customer_id, customer_type, first_name, last_name, phone FROM customer WHERE customer_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", customerId);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        return await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken)
            ? new CustomerForm
            {
                CustomerId = reader.GetInt32(0),
                CustomerType = reader.GetString(1),
                FirstName = reader.GetString(2),
                LastName = reader.GetString(3),
                Phone = reader.GetString(4)
            }
            : null;
    }

    public async Task<int> CreateAsync(CustomerForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "INSERT INTO customer (customer_type, first_name, last_name, phone) VALUES (@type, @first_name, @last_name, @phone) RETURNING customer_id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        AddParameters(command, form);
        return (int)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? 0);
    }

    public async Task UpdateAsync(CustomerForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "UPDATE customer SET customer_type = @type, first_name = @first_name, last_name = @last_name, phone = @phone WHERE customer_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        AddParameters(command, form);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", form.CustomerId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task DeleteAsync(int customerId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("DELETE FROM customer WHERE customer_id = @id", connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", customerId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private static void AddParameters(NpgsqlCommand command, CustomerForm form)
    {
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("type", form.CustomerType);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("first_name", form.FirstName.Trim());
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("last_name", form.LastName.Trim());
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("phone", form.Phone.Trim());
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Data\Repositories\EmployeeRepository.cs =====
using Npgsql;
using NpgsqlTypes;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;

public sealed class EmployeeRepository(NpgsqlDataSource dataSource) : IEmployeeRepository
{
    public async Task<Employee?> FindByLoginAsync(string login, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT employee_id, first_name, last_name, role, login, password_hash FROM employee WHERE login = @login";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("login", login);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        return await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken) ? ReadEmployee(reader) : null;
    }

    public async Task<Employee?> GetAsync(int employeeId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT employee_id, first_name, last_name, role, login, password_hash FROM employee WHERE employee_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", employeeId);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        return await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken) ? ReadEmployee(reader) : null;
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<Employee>> GetAllAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "SELECT employee_id, first_name, last_name, role, login, password_hash FROM employee ORDER BY last_name, first_name";
        var employees = new List<Employee>();
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        while (await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken))
        {
            employees.Add(ReadEmployee(reader));
        }

        return employees;
    }

    public async Task<int> CreateAsync(Employee employee, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "INSERT INTO employee (first_name, last_name, role, login, password_hash) VALUES (@first_name, @last_name, @role, @login, @password_hash) RETURNING employee_id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        AddEmployeeParameters(command, employee);
        return (int)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? 0);
    }

    public async Task UpdateAsync(Employee employee, string? passwordHash, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "UPDATE employee SET first_name = @first_name, last_name = @last_name, role = @role, login = @login, password_hash = COALESCE(@password_hash, password_hash) WHERE employee_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        AddEmployeeParameters(command, employee);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("password_hash", NpgsqlDbType.Varchar, (object?)passwordHash ?? DBNull.Value);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", employee.EmployeeId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task DeleteAsync(int employeeId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("DELETE FROM employee WHERE employee_id = @id", connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", employeeId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private static Employee ReadEmployee(NpgsqlDataReader reader)
    {
        return new Employee
        {
            EmployeeId = reader.GetInt32(0),
            FirstName = reader.GetString(1),
            LastName = reader.GetString(2),
            Role = reader.GetString(3),
            Login = reader.GetString(4),
            PasswordHash = reader.GetString(5)
        };
    }

    private static void AddEmployeeParameters(NpgsqlCommand command, Employee employee)
    {
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("first_name", employee.FirstName);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("last_name", employee.LastName);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("role", employee.Role);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("login", employee.Login);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("password_hash", employee.PasswordHash);
    }
}

===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Data\Repositories\OrderRepository.cs =====
using Npgsql;
using NpgsqlTypes;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;

public sealed class OrderRepository(NpgsqlDataSource dataSource) : IOrderRepository
{
    private const string SummarySelect = "SELECT o.order_id, o.car_id, concat(c.model, ', ', c.license_plate), concat_ws(' ', cu.last_name, cu.first_name), concat_ws(' ', e.last_name, e.first_name), o.order_date, o.deadline, o.status, o.total_cost FROM orders o JOIN car c ON c.car_id = o.car_id JOIN customer cu ON cu.customer_id = c.customer_id JOIN employee e ON e.employee_id = o.employee_id";

    public Task<IReadOnlyList<OrderSummary>> GetAllAsync(string? query, string? status, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = SummarySelect + " WHERE (@query = '' OR c.model ILIKE @pattern OR c.license_plate ILIKE @pattern OR cu.first_name ILIKE @pattern OR cu.last_name ILIKE @pattern) AND (@status = '' OR o.status = @status) ORDER BY o.order_date DESC, o.order_id DESC";
        return GetSummariesAsync(sql, command =>
        {
            var search = query?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
            command.Parameters.AddWithValue("query", search);
            command.Parameters.AddWithValue("pattern", $"%{search}%");
            command.Parameters.AddWithValue("status", status?.Trim() ?? string.Empty);
        }, cancellationToken);
    }

    public Task<IReadOnlyList<OrderSummary>> GetCarHistoryAsync(int carId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return GetSummariesAsync(SummarySelect + " WHERE o.car_id = @car_id ORDER BY o.order_date DESC, o.order_id DESC", command => command.Parameters.AddWithValue("car_id", carId), cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task<OrderDetails?> GetDetailsAsync(int orderId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string orderSql = "SELECT o.order_id, o.car_id, concat(c.model, ', ', c.license_plate), concat_ws(' ', cu.last_name, cu.first_name), cu.phone, concat_ws(' ', e.last_name, e.first_name), o.order_date, o.deadline, o.status, o.mileage, o.discount, o.total_cost FROM orders o JOIN car c ON c.car_id = o.car_id JOIN customer cu ON cu.customer_id = c.customer_id JOIN employee e ON e.employee_id = o.employee_id WHERE o.order_id = @id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        OrderDetails? details;
        await using (var command = new NpgsqlCommand(orderSql, connection))
        {
            command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
            await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
            if (!await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken))
            {
                return null;
            }

            details = new OrderDetails
            {
                OrderId = reader.GetInt32(0),
                CarId = reader.GetInt32(1),
                Car = reader.GetString(2),
                Customer = reader.GetString(3),
                Phone = reader.GetString(4),
                Employee = reader.GetString(5),
                OrderDate = reader.GetFieldValue<DateOnly>(6),
                Deadline = reader.IsDBNull(7) ? null : reader.GetFieldValue<DateOnly>(7),
                Status = reader.GetString(8),
                Mileage = reader.GetInt32(9),
                Discount = reader.GetDecimal(10),
                TotalCost = reader.GetDecimal(11)
            };
        }

        details.Services.AddRange(await GetLinesAsync(connection, orderId, false, cancellationToken));
        details.Parts.AddRange(await GetLinesAsync(connection, orderId, true, cancellationToken));
        return details;
    }

    public async Task<int> CreateAsync(OrderCreateForm form, int employeeId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        const string sql = "INSERT INTO orders (car_id, employee_id, order_date, deadline, status, mileage) VALUES (@car_id, @employee_id, @order_date, @deadline, 'open', @mileage) RETURNING order_id";
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("car_id", form.CarId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("employee_id", employeeId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("order_date", form.OrderDate);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("deadline", NpgsqlDbType.Date, (object?)form.Deadline ?? DBNull.Value);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("mileage", form.Mileage);
        return (int)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? 0);
    }

    public async Task UpdateAsync(OrderEditForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await EnsureOpenAsync(connection, transaction, form.OrderId, cancellationToken);
        const string sql = "UPDATE orders SET car_id = @car_id, order_date = @order_date, deadline = @deadline, mileage = @mileage WHERE order_id = @id";
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("car_id", form.CarId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("order_date", form.OrderDate);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("deadline", NpgsqlDbType.Date, (object?)form.Deadline ?? DBNull.Value);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("mileage", form.Mileage);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", form.OrderId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
        await transaction.CommitAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task DeleteAsync(int orderId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await EnsureDraftAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("DELETE FROM orders WHERE order_id = @id", connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
        await transaction.CommitAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task AddServiceAsync(int orderId, AddServiceForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await EnsureOpenAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        const string sql = "INSERT INTO order_service (order_id, service_id, employee_id, quantity, price) SELECT @order_id, s.service_id, @employee_id, @quantity, s.price FROM service s WHERE s.service_id = @service_id ON CONFLICT (order_id, service_id) DO UPDATE SET quantity = order_service.quantity + EXCLUDED.quantity, employee_id = EXCLUDED.employee_id";
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("order_id", orderId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("service_id", form.ServiceId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("employee_id", form.EmployeeId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("quantity", form.Quantity);
        if (await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken) == 0)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException("Послугу не знайдено.");
        }

        await MarkInProgressAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await RecalculateAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await transaction.CommitAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task AddPartAsync(int orderId, AddPartForm form, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await EnsureOpenAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        const string sql = "INSERT INTO order_part (order_id, part_id, quantity, price) SELECT @order_id, p.part_id, @quantity, p.price FROM part p WHERE p.part_id = @part_id ON CONFLICT (order_id, part_id) DO UPDATE SET quantity = order_part.quantity + EXCLUDED.quantity";
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("order_id", orderId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("part_id", form.PartId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("quantity", form.Quantity);
        if (await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken) == 0)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException("Запчастину не знайдено.");
        }

        await MarkInProgressAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await RecalculateAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await transaction.CommitAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public Task RemoveServiceAsync(int orderId, int serviceId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return RemoveLineAsync(orderId, serviceId, false, cancellationToken);
    }

    public Task RemovePartAsync(int orderId, int partId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return RemoveLineAsync(orderId, partId, true, cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task UpdateDiscountAsync(int orderId, decimal discount, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await EnsureOpenAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await using (var command = new NpgsqlCommand("UPDATE orders SET discount = @discount WHERE order_id = @id", connection, transaction))
        {
            command.Parameters.AddWithValue("discount", discount);
            command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
            await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
        }

        await RecalculateAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await transaction.CommitAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task CloseAsync(int orderId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await EnsureOpenAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await using (var check = new NpgsqlCommand("SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM order_service WHERE order_id = @id)", connection, transaction))
        {
            check.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
            if ((bool)(await check.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? false) is false)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Додайте щонайменше одну послугу перед закриттям замовлення.");
            }
        }

        await RecalculateAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("UPDATE orders SET status = 'closed' WHERE order_id = @id", connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
        await transaction.CommitAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private async Task<IReadOnlyList<OrderSummary>> GetSummariesAsync(string sql, Action<NpgsqlCommand> addParameters, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var orders = new List<OrderSummary>();
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        addParameters(command);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        while (await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken))
        {
            orders.Add(new OrderSummary
            {
                OrderId = reader.GetInt32(0),
                CarId = reader.GetInt32(1),
                Car = reader.GetString(2),
                Customer = reader.GetString(3),
                Employee = reader.GetString(4),
                OrderDate = reader.GetFieldValue<DateOnly>(5),
                Deadline = reader.IsDBNull(6) ? null : reader.GetFieldValue<DateOnly>(6),
                Status = reader.GetString(7),
                TotalCost = reader.GetDecimal(8)
            });
        }

        return orders;
    }

    private static async Task<List<OrderLine>> GetLinesAsync(NpgsqlConnection connection, int orderId, bool parts, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var sql = parts
            ? "SELECT p.part_id, p.name, p.article, op.quantity, op.price, '', true FROM order_part op JOIN part p ON p.part_id = op.part_id WHERE op.order_id = @id ORDER BY p.name"
            : "SELECT s.service_id, s.name, '', os.quantity, os.price, concat_ws(' ', e.last_name, e.first_name), false FROM order_service os JOIN service s ON s.service_id = os.service_id JOIN employee e ON e.employee_id = os.employee_id WHERE os.order_id = @id ORDER BY s.name";
        var lines = new List<OrderLine>();
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(cancellationToken);
        while (await reader.ReadAsync(cancellationToken))
        {
            lines.Add(new OrderLine
            {
                ItemId = reader.GetInt32(0),
                Name = reader.GetString(1),
                Article = reader.GetString(2),
                Quantity = reader.GetInt32(3),
                Price = reader.GetDecimal(4),
                Employee = reader.GetString(5),
                IsPart = reader.GetBoolean(6)
            });
        }

        return lines;
    }

    private async Task RemoveLineAsync(int orderId, int itemId, bool part, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await EnsureOpenAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        var table = part ? "order_part" : "order_service";
        var key = part ? "part_id" : "service_id";
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand($"DELETE FROM {table} WHERE order_id = @order_id AND {key} = @item_id", connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("order_id", orderId);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("item_id", itemId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
        await RecalculateAsync(connection, transaction, orderId, cancellationToken);
        await transaction.CommitAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private static async Task EnsureOpenAsync(NpgsqlConnection connection, NpgsqlTransaction transaction, int orderId, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("SELECT status FROM orders WHERE order_id = @id FOR UPDATE", connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
        var status = await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) as string;
        if (status is null)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException("Замовлення не знайдено.");
        }

        if (status == "closed")
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Закрите замовлення не можна змінити.");
        }
    }

    private static async Task EnsureDraftAsync(NpgsqlConnection connection, NpgsqlTransaction transaction, int orderId, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("SELECT status FROM orders WHERE order_id = @id FOR UPDATE", connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
        var status = await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) as string;
        if (status is null)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException("Замовлення не знайдено.");
        }

        if (status != "open")
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Видалити можна лише нове замовлення без виконаних робіт.");
        }
    }

    private static async Task RecalculateAsync(NpgsqlConnection connection, NpgsqlTransaction transaction, int orderId, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        const string sql = "UPDATE orders o SET total_cost = ROUND(((SELECT COALESCE(SUM(quantity * price), 0) FROM order_service WHERE order_id = o.order_id) + (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(quantity * price), 0) FROM order_part WHERE order_id = o.order_id)) * (100 - o.discount) / 100, 2) WHERE o.order_id = @id";
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand(sql, connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private static async Task MarkInProgressAsync(NpgsqlConnection connection, NpgsqlTransaction transaction, int orderId, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("UPDATE orders SET status = 'in_progress' WHERE order_id = @id AND status = 'open'", connection, transaction);
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", orderId);
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }
}
