===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Infrastructure\CommandFileRunner.cs =====
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;

public sealed class CommandFileRunner(ILogger<CommandFileRunner> logger)
{
    public async Task RunAsync(string commandName, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var commandDirectory = FindCommandDirectory();
        var scriptPath = OperatingSystem.IsWindows()
            ? Path.Combine(commandDirectory, "windows", $"{commandName}.ps1")
            : Path.Combine(commandDirectory, "linux", $"{commandName}.sh");

        if (!File.Exists(scriptPath))
        {
            throw new FileNotFoundException($"Database command file was not found: {scriptPath}");
        }

        var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
        {
            FileName = OperatingSystem.IsWindows() ? "powershell.exe" : "bash",
            WorkingDirectory = commandDirectory,
            RedirectStandardOutput = true,
            RedirectStandardError = true,
            UseShellExecute = false,
            CreateNoWindow = true
        };

        if (OperatingSystem.IsWindows())
        {
            startInfo.ArgumentList.Add("-ExecutionPolicy");
            startInfo.ArgumentList.Add("Bypass");
            startInfo.ArgumentList.Add("-NoProfile");
            startInfo.ArgumentList.Add("-NonInteractive");
            startInfo.ArgumentList.Add("-File");
            startInfo.ArgumentList.Add(scriptPath);
        }
        else
        {
            startInfo.ArgumentList.Add(scriptPath);
        }

        using var process = Process.Start(startInfo) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Could not start the database command process.");
        using var timeout = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(cancellationToken);
        timeout.CancelAfter(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2));

        var outputTask = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync(timeout.Token);
        var errorTask = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync(timeout.Token);
        try
        {
            await process.WaitForExitAsync(timeout.Token);
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
            process.Kill(true);
            throw new TimeoutException($"Database command '{commandName}' exceeded its two minute limit.");
        }

        var output = await outputTask;
        var error = await errorTask;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(output))
        {
            logger.LogInformation("Database command {CommandName}: {CommandOutput}", commandName, output.Trim());
        }

        if (process.ExitCode != 0)
        {
            var details = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(error) ? output : error;
            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Database command '{commandName}' failed: {details.Trim()}");
        }
    }

    public async Task RunSqlFileAsync(string fileName, DatabaseSettings settings, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var commandDirectory = FindCommandDirectory();
        var sqlPath = Path.Combine(commandDirectory, "sql", fileName);
        if (!File.Exists(sqlPath))
        {
            throw new FileNotFoundException($"Database SQL file was not found: {sqlPath}");
        }

        var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
        {
            FileName = "psql",
            WorkingDirectory = commandDirectory,
            RedirectStandardOutput = true,
            RedirectStandardError = true,
            UseShellExecute = false,
            CreateNoWindow = true
        };
        startInfo.Environment["PGPASSWORD"] = settings.AppPassword;
        foreach (var argument in new[] { "-X", "-w", "--set", "ON_ERROR_STOP=1", "--host", settings.Host, "--port", settings.Port.ToString(), "--username", settings.AppUser, "--dbname", settings.Database, "--file", sqlPath })
        {
            startInfo.ArgumentList.Add(argument);
        }

        using var process = Process.Start(startInfo) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Could not start psql.");
        using var timeout = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(cancellationToken);
        timeout.CancelAfter(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2));
        var outputTask = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync(timeout.Token);
        var errorTask = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync(timeout.Token);
        try
        {
            await process.WaitForExitAsync(timeout.Token);
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
            process.Kill(true);
            throw new TimeoutException($"SQL command '{fileName}' exceeded its two minute limit.");
        }

        var output = await outputTask;
        var error = await errorTask;
        if (process.ExitCode != 0)
        {
            var details = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(error) ? output : error;
            throw new InvalidOperationException($"SQL command '{fileName}' failed: {details.Trim()}");
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(output))
        {
            logger.LogInformation("SQL command {SqlFile}: {CommandOutput}", fileName, output.Trim());
        }
    }

    private static string FindCommandDirectory()
    {
        foreach (var start in new[] { Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), AppContext.BaseDirectory })
        {
            var directory = new DirectoryInfo(start);
            while (directory is not null)
            {
                var sourceCommands = Path.Combine(directory.FullName, "commands");
                if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(sourceCommands, "windows", "create-database.ps1")) || File.Exists(Path.Combine(sourceCommands, "linux", "create-database.sh")))
                {
                    return sourceCommands;
                }

                if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(directory.FullName, "commands", "sql", "schema.sql")))
                {
                    return Path.Combine(directory.FullName, "commands");
                }

                directory = directory.Parent;
            }
        }

        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("The commands folder was not found beside the application or in its parent directories.");
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Infrastructure\DatabaseBootstrapper.cs =====
using Npgsql;
using NpgsqlTypes;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;

public sealed class DatabaseBootstrapper(
    DatabaseSettings settings,
    NpgsqlDataSource dataSource,
    CommandFileRunner commandRunner,
    ILogger<DatabaseBootstrapper> logger)
{
    private static readonly SemaphoreSlim Gate = new(1, 1);

    public async Task InitializeAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await Gate.WaitAsync(cancellationToken);
        try
        {
            await EnsureDatabaseExistsAsync(cancellationToken);
            await EnsureSchemaExistsAsync(cancellationToken);
            await EnsureApplicationPermissionsAsync(cancellationToken);
            await EnsureSeedDataAsync(cancellationToken);
            await EnsureInitialAdministratorAsync(cancellationToken);
            await EnsureDemoPasswordsAsync(cancellationToken);
            await VerifyApplicationConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            logger.LogCritical(exception, "The application could not initialize or connect to its database.");
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Не вдалося підготувати базу даних. Перевірте доступність PostgreSQL, значення в .env та наявність psql у PATH.", exception);
        }
        finally
        {
            Gate.Release();
        }
    }

    private async Task EnsureDatabaseExistsAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        try
        {
            await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        }
        catch (PostgresException exception) when (exception.SqlState is "3D000" or "28P01")
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.AdminUser) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.AdminPassword) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.MaintenanceDatabase))
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Set PG_ADMIN_USER, PG_ADMIN_PASSWORD and PGMAINTENANCE_DB in .env so the application can create the database or initialize its application account.", exception);
            }

            logger.LogInformation("Database or application account needs initialization. Running database creation command.");
            await commandRunner.RunAsync("create-database", cancellationToken);
            await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        }
    }

    private async Task EnsureSchemaExistsAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("SELECT to_regclass('public.customer') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.employee') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.car') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.orders') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.service') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.part') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.order_service') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.order_part') IS NOT NULL", connection);
        var exists = (bool)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? false);
        if (!exists)
        {
            logger.LogInformation("Database schema is incomplete. Applying schema.sql.");
            await commandRunner.RunSqlFileAsync("schema.sql", settings, cancellationToken);
        }
    }

    private async Task EnsureSeedDataAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM customer UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM employee UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM car UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM orders UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM service UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM part UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM order_service UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM order_part)", connection);
        var hasData = (bool)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? false);
        if (!hasData)
        {
            logger.LogInformation("Database has no records. Running database population command.");
            await commandRunner.RunAsync("populate-database", cancellationToken);
        }
    }

    private async Task EnsureApplicationPermissionsAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("SELECT has_table_privilege(current_user, 'customer', 'SELECT')", connection);
        var hasPermission = (bool)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? false);
        if (hasPermission)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.AdminUser) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.AdminPassword) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.MaintenanceDatabase))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException("The application database user lacks table permissions. Set administrator credentials in .env to initialize them.");
        }

        logger.LogInformation("Application database permissions are missing. Running database initialization command.");
        await commandRunner.RunAsync("create-database", cancellationToken);
    }

    private async Task EnsureInitialAdministratorAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM employee WHERE role = 'admin')", connection);
        var hasAdministrator = (bool)(await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken) ?? false);
        if (hasAdministrator)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.InitialAdminLogin) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.InitialAdminPassword))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Set INITIAL_ADMIN_LOGIN and INITIAL_ADMIN_PASSWORD to create the first administrator.");
        }

        var hasher = new Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity.PasswordHasher<Models.Employee>();
        var administrator = new Models.Employee
        {
            FirstName = "System",
            LastName = "Administrator",
            Role = "admin",
            Login = settings.InitialAdminLogin
        };
        administrator.PasswordHash = hasher.HashPassword(administrator, settings.InitialAdminPassword);

        await using var insert = new NpgsqlCommand("INSERT INTO employee (first_name, last_name, role, login, password_hash) VALUES (@first_name, @last_name, 'admin', @login, @password_hash) ON CONFLICT (login) DO NOTHING", connection);
        insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("first_name", administrator.FirstName);
        insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("last_name", administrator.LastName);
        insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("login", administrator.Login);
        insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("password_hash", administrator.PasswordHash);
        await insert.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private async Task EnsureDemoPasswordsAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        var accounts = new[]
        {
            (Login: "demo.manager", Password: settings.DemoManagerPassword),
            (Login: "demo.mechanic", Password: settings.DemoMechanicPassword),
            (Login: "demo.director", Password: settings.DemoDirectorPassword)
        };
        var hasher = new Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity.PasswordHasher<Models.Employee>();

        foreach (var account in accounts)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(account.Password))
            {
                continue;
            }

            var employee = new Models.Employee { Login = account.Login };
            var passwordHash = hasher.HashPassword(employee, account.Password);
            await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("UPDATE employee SET password_hash = @password_hash WHERE login = @login AND password_hash = '!'", connection);
            command.Parameters.AddWithValue("login", account.Login);
            command.Parameters.AddWithValue("password_hash", passwordHash);
            await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(cancellationToken);
        }
    }

    private async Task VerifyApplicationConnectionAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await using var connection = await dataSource.OpenConnectionAsync(cancellationToken);
        await using var command = new NpgsqlCommand("SELECT 1", connection);
        await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(cancellationToken);
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Infrastructure\DatabaseSettings.cs =====
using Npgsql;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;

public sealed class DatabaseSettings
{
    public string Host { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public int Port { get; init; }
    public string Database { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string MaintenanceDatabase { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string AdminUser { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string AdminPassword { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string AppUser { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string AppPassword { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string InitialAdminLogin { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string InitialAdminPassword { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string DemoManagerPassword { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string DemoMechanicPassword { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string DemoDirectorPassword { get; init; } = string.Empty;

    public static DatabaseSettings Load()
    {
        return new DatabaseSettings
        {
            Host = Required("PGHOST"),
            Port = int.TryParse(Required("PGPORT"), out var port) && port is > 0 and < 65536
                ? port
                : throw new InvalidOperationException("PGPORT must be a valid TCP port."),
            Database = Required("PGDATABASE"),
            MaintenanceDatabase = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PGMAINTENANCE_DB") ?? string.Empty,
            AdminUser = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PG_ADMIN_USER") ?? string.Empty,
            AdminPassword = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PG_ADMIN_PASSWORD") ?? string.Empty,
            AppUser = Required("APP_DB_USER"),
            AppPassword = Required("APP_DB_PASSWORD"),
            InitialAdminLogin = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("INITIAL_ADMIN_LOGIN") ?? string.Empty,
            InitialAdminPassword = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("INITIAL_ADMIN_PASSWORD") ?? string.Empty,
            DemoManagerPassword = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("DEMO_MANAGER_PASSWORD") ?? string.Empty,
            DemoMechanicPassword = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("DEMO_MECHANIC_PASSWORD") ?? string.Empty,
            DemoDirectorPassword = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("DEMO_DIRECTOR_PASSWORD") ?? string.Empty
        };
    }

    public string BuildAdminConnectionString(string database)
    {
        return new NpgsqlConnectionStringBuilder
        {
            Host = Host,
            Port = Port,
            Database = database,
            Username = AdminUser,
            Password = AdminPassword,
            Timeout = 5,
            CommandTimeout = 15,
            ApplicationName = "VehicleServiceStation Bootstrap"
        }.ConnectionString;
    }

    public string BuildApplicationConnectionString()
    {
        return new NpgsqlConnectionStringBuilder
        {
            Host = Host,
            Port = Port,
            Database = Database,
            Username = AppUser,
            Password = AppPassword,
            Timeout = 5,
            CommandTimeout = 15,
            ApplicationName = "VehicleServiceStation"
        }.ConnectionString;
    }

    private static string Required(string key)
    {
        var value = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(key);
        return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)
            ? throw new InvalidOperationException($"Set {key} in the environment or local .env file.")
            : value;
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Infrastructure\DataErrorController.cs =====
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Npgsql;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;

public abstract class DataErrorController : Controller
{
    protected void AddDataError(Exception exception)
    {
        var postgres = exception as PostgresException ?? exception.InnerException as PostgresException;
        var message = postgres?.SqlState switch
        {
            PostgresErrorCodes.UniqueViolation => "Такий запис уже існує. Перевірте унікальні значення.",
            PostgresErrorCodes.ForeignKeyViolation => "Запис використовується в замовленнях або інших даних і не може бути видалений.",
            PostgresErrorCodes.CheckViolation => "Значення не відповідає обмеженням бази даних.",
            PostgresErrorCodes.NotNullViolation => "Заповніть усі обов’язкові поля.",
            _ => exception is KeyNotFoundException or InvalidOperationException ? exception.Message : "Не вдалося зберегти зміни. Перевірте дані та спробуйте ще раз."
        };

        ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty, message);
    }
}


===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Infrastructure\EnvironmentFile.cs =====
namespace VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;

public static class EnvironmentFile
{
    public static void Load()
    {
        foreach (var start in new[] { Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), AppContext.BaseDirectory })
        {
            var root = FindRoot(start);
            if (root is null)
            {
                continue;
            }

            var file = Path.Combine(root, ".env");
            if (!File.Exists(file))
            {
                continue;
            }

            foreach (var line in File.ReadLines(file))
            {
                var entry = line.Trim();
                if (entry.Length == 0 || entry.StartsWith('#'))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                var separator = entry.IndexOf('=');
                if (separator < 1)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                var key = entry[..separator].Trim();
                var value = entry[(separator + 1)..].Trim();
                if (value.Length >= 2 && (value[0] == '"' && value[^1] == '"' || value[0] == '\'' && value[^1] == '\''))
                {
                    value = value[1..^1];
                }

                if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(key) is null)
                {
                    Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(key, value);
                }
            }

            return;
        }
    }

    private static string? FindRoot(string path)
    {
        var directory = new DirectoryInfo(path);
        while (directory is not null)
        {
            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(directory.FullName, ".env")) || Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(directory.FullName, "commands")))
            {
                return directory.FullName;
            }

            directory = directory.Parent;
        }

        return null;
    }
}

===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Models\Forms\OrderForms.cs =====
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

namespace VehicleServiceStation.Models.Forms;

public sealed class OrderCreateForm : IValidatableObject
{
    [Range(1, int.MaxValue, ErrorMessage = "Оберіть автомобіль.")]
    public int CarId { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateOnly OrderDate { get; set; } = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

    public DateOnly? Deadline { get; set; }

    [Range(0, int.MaxValue, ErrorMessage = "Пробіг не може бути від’ємним.")]
    public int Mileage { get; set; }

    public IEnumerable<ValidationResult> Validate(ValidationContext validationContext)
    {
        if (Deadline.HasValue && Deadline.Value < OrderDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult("Термін не може бути раніше дати замовлення.", [nameof(Deadline)]);
        }
    }
}

public sealed class OrderEditForm : IValidatableObject
{
    public int OrderId { get; set; }

    [Range(1, int.MaxValue, ErrorMessage = "Оберіть автомобіль.")]
    public int CarId { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateOnly OrderDate { get; set; }

    public DateOnly? Deadline { get; set; }

    [Range(0, int.MaxValue, ErrorMessage = "Пробіг не може бути від’ємним.")]
    public int Mileage { get; set; }

    public IEnumerable<ValidationResult> Validate(ValidationContext validationContext)
    {
        if (Deadline.HasValue && Deadline.Value < OrderDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult("Термін не може бути раніше дати замовлення.", [nameof(Deadline)]);
        }
    }
}

===== ФАЙЛ: MVC\VehicleServiceStation\Program.cs =====
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.Cookies;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Npgsql;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data;
using VehicleServiceStation.Data.Repositories;
using VehicleServiceStation.Infrastructure;
using VehicleServiceStation.Models;

EnvironmentFile.Load();
var databaseSettings = DatabaseSettings.Load();
var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

builder.Services.AddSingleton(databaseSettings);
builder.Services.AddSingleton(_ => NpgsqlDataSource.Create(databaseSettings.BuildApplicationConnectionString()));
builder.Services.AddSingleton<CommandFileRunner>();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<DatabaseBootstrapper>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IPasswordHasher<Employee>, PasswordHasher<Employee>>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IEmployeeRepository, EmployeeRepository>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<ICustomerRepository, CustomerRepository>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<ICarRepository, CarRepository>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<ICatalogRepository, CatalogRepository>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IOrderRepository, OrderRepository>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<FormOptionsService>();
builder.Services.AddAuthentication(CookieAuthenticationDefaults.AuthenticationScheme)
    .AddCookie(options =>
    {
        options.LoginPath = "/Account/Login";
        options.AccessDeniedPath = "/Home/AccessDenied";
        options.Cookie.Name = "VehicleServiceStation.Auth";
        options.Cookie.HttpOnly = true;
        options.Cookie.SameSite = SameSiteMode.Lax;
        options.Cookie.SecurePolicy = CookieSecurePolicy.SameAsRequest;
        options.SlidingExpiration = true;
        options.ExpireTimeSpan = TimeSpan.FromHours(8);
    });
builder.Services.AddAuthorization();
builder.Services.AddControllersWithViews(options => options.Filters.Add(new AutoValidateAntiforgeryTokenAttribute()));

var app = builder.Build();
await app.Services.GetRequiredService<DatabaseBootstrapper>().InitializeAsync(app.Lifetime.ApplicationStopping);

if (!app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseExceptionHandler("/Home/Error");
    app.UseHsts();
}

app.UseHttpsRedirection();
app.UseStaticFiles();
app.UseRouting();
app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseAuthorization();
app.MapControllerRoute(
    name: "default",
    pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}");

app.Run();

    ViewData["Title"] = "Послуги";

<section class="page-card"><div class="card-toolbar"><div><strong>Доступні послуги</strong><span class="count-pill">@Model.Count</span></div><span class="muted">Імпорт також доступний через командний файл</span></div>
@if (Model.Count == 0)
{
    <div class="table-empty"><span>✳</span><strong>Каталог послуг порожній</strong><p>Додайте послугу або скористайтеся командою імпорту.</p></div>
}
else
{
    <div class="table-responsive"><table class="data-table"><thead><tr><th>ПОСЛУГА</th><th>ЦІНА</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>@foreach (var item in Model){<tr><td><strong>@item.Name</strong><small class="cell-sub">Код @item.Id.ToString("D4")</small></td><td class="money-cell">@item.Price.ToString("N2") ₴</td><td class="row-actions"><a class="text-link" asp-action="Edit" asp-route-id="@item.Id">Редагувати</a><form asp-action="Delete" asp-route-id="@item.Id" method="post"><button class="text-link text-danger" type="submit">Видалити</button></form></td></tr>}</tbody></table></div>
}
</section>

</p>
<p>
    <strong>The Development environment shouldn't be enabled for deployed applications.</strong>
    It can result in displaying sensitive information from exceptions to end users.
    For local debugging, enable the <strong>Development</strong> environment by setting the <strong>ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT</strong> environment variable to <strong>Development</strong>
    and restarting the app.
</p>


===== ФАЙЛ: README.md =====
# Команди бази даних

Перед першим запуском заповніть `PG_ADMIN_PASSWORD` у `.env`.

Windows PowerShell:

```powershell
.\commands\windows\create-database.ps1
.\commands\windows\populate-database.ps1
.\commands\windows\drop-database.ps1
```

Linux:

```bash
bash commands/linux/create-database.sh
bash commands/linux/populate-database.sh
bash commands/linux/drop-database.sh
```
